   816	// ===========================================================================
   817	// H1 (B1) — the WRITER prompt must carry the verdict-tail contract.
   818	describe('pipeline — H1: writer prompt carries the verdict-tail contract', () => {
   819	  it('RED (H1): the writer prompt contains "End with exactly" and "VERDICT:" (a writer with no verdict parses BLOCKED-unparseable)', async () => {
   820	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
   821	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
   822	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   823	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   824	    const writer = writersOf(io.__runner)[0];
   825	    assert.ok(writer, 'a writer child ran');
   826	    const prompt = argsOf(writer).join(' ');
   827	    assert.match(prompt, /End with exactly/, 'the writer prompt states the required tail');
   828	    assert.match(prompt, /VERDICT:/, 'the writer prompt names the VERDICT line it must emit');
   829	  });
   830	});
   831	
   832	// ===========================================================================
   833	// H2 (A1/B2) — bounded failover: avoidEntries/avoid carry; exhaustion PARKS.
   834	describe('pipeline — H2: bounded failover (avoid carries; exhaustion parks)', () => {
   835	  // A recording runner that emits a platform-appropriate death banner and THROWS
   836	  // once a bound is exceeded, so an unbounded ping-pong terminates the test RED.
   837	  function boundedDeathRunner(io, { bound, banner }) {
   838	    const calls = [];
   839	    const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts) => {
   840	      calls.push({ spec, command: spec?.command, args: (spec?.args ?? []).map(String) });
   841	      if (calls.length > bound) throw new Error(`unbounded failover: exceeded ${bound} child spawns (ping-pong)`);
   842	      const text = typeof banner === 'function' ? banner(spec) : banner;
   843	      io.fs.mkdirSync(opts.logPath.slice(0, opts.logPath.lastIndexOf('/')), { recursive: true });
   844	      io.fs.writeFileSync(opts.logPath, `working...\n${text}\n`);
   845	      return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: 1, verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: '', logPath: opts.logPath });
   846	    };
   847	    fn.calls = calls;
   848	    return fn;
   849	  }
   850	  const modelsOf = (runner) => runner.calls.filter((c) => valAfter(c.args, '-s') === 'workspace-write').map((c) => valAfter(c.args, '--model'));
   851	
   852	  it('RED (H2a): always-model-unavailable on a two-entry codex chain never re-picks a rejected model; all rejected -> PARK (bounded)', async () => {
   853	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['codex/wa1@xhigh', 'codex/wa2@xhigh'] } };
   854	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), config, runner: undefined });
   855	    const runner = boundedDeathRunner(io, { bound: 3, banner: MODEL_UNAVAIL }); // correct impl parks at 2 writers
   856	    io.superviseChild = runner;
   857	    io.__runner = runner;
   858	    let code = 'ran';
   859	    try { code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io); } catch { code = 'unbounded'; }
   860	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'both models rejected -> the subtask parks (bounded, no infinite ping-pong)');
   861	    // BOTH entries are attempted, in order, exactly once — then it parks.
   862	    assert.deepEqual(modelsOf(runner), ['wa1', 'wa2'], `each chain entry is tried once, in order; got ${JSON.stringify(modelsOf(runner))}`);
   863	    assert.ok(runner.calls.length <= 3, `the runner stayed within the throwing bound (no ping-pong); calls=${runner.calls.length}`);
   864	  });
   865	
   866	  it('RED (H2b): always-usage-limit across a cross-platform chain parks within a bounded child budget (no infinite loop)', async () => {
   867	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['claude-code/wa-cc', 'codex/wa-cx@xhigh'] } };
   868	    const io = makePipeRepo({
   869	      spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }),
   870	      config,
   871	      files: { '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': JSON.stringify(ownedBundle('claude-code', 'open'), null, 2) + '\n' },
   872	      runner: undefined,
   873	    });
   874	    const runner = boundedDeathRunner(io, { bound: 4, banner: (spec) => (spec.command === 'claude' ? CC_LIMIT : CODEX_LIMIT) });
   875	    io.superviseChild = runner;
   876	    io.__runner = runner;
   877	    let code = 'ran';
   878	    try { code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io); } catch { code = 'unbounded'; }
   879	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a repeatedly-limit-dying subtask parks within the child budget, never loops forever');
   880	  });
   881	});
   882	
   883	// ===========================================================================
   884	// D2 (dogfood milestone C) — `pipeline run` must actually PASS the main repo
   885	// .git root to write-capable writer children: a linked worktree's index/lock
   886	// live under <mainRoot>/.git/worktrees/<seat>, outside the seat-cwd sandbox
   887	// (live child 002 could not commit: "sandbox only has read access to
   888	// .git/worktrees/wt-a/index.lock"). The unit pin proves buildChildArgv honors
   889	// opts.gitDir; THIS pin proves the command wires it through (pipeline.mjs:237
   890	// still passes only {root: seatPath}).
   891	describe('pipeline — D2: the writer spawn passes the main .git as a sandbox writable root', () => {
   892	  // Every value that follows a `-c` flag in a codex argv (config args).
   893	  const configArgs = (args) => args.map((a, i) => (a === '-c' ? args[i + 1] : null)).filter((v) => typeof v === 'string');
   894	
   895	  it('RED (D2): the write-capable writer child runs in its SEAT cwd yet gets the MAIN /repo/.git in sandbox_workspace_write.writable_roots', async () => {
   896	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
   897	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
   898	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   899	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   900	
   901	    const writer = writersOf(io.__runner)[0];
   902	    assert.ok(writer, 'a write-capable writer child ran');
   903	    const args = argsOf(writer);
   904	    // The cwd stays the seat worktree (codex passes it via -C) — the sandbox is
   905	    // still rooted at the seat, the .git dir is only an ADDITIONAL writable root.
   906	    assert.equal(valAfter(args, '-C'), WT_A, 'the writer runs in its seat worktree, not the main root');
   907	    const wr = configArgs(args).find((v) => /sandbox_workspace_write\.writable_roots/.test(String(v)));
   908	    assert.ok(wr, 'the writer argv carries a -c sandbox_workspace_write.writable_roots grant');
   909	    assert.match(String(wr), /\/repo\/\.git/, 'the extra writable root is the MAIN repo .git dir (where the linked-worktree index/lock live)');
   910	  });
   911	});
   912	
   913	// ===========================================================================
   914	// D1 (dogfood milestone C) — classification reads the transcript TAIL, not the
   915	// whole log: a child's OWN work product (a README diff hunk that DOCUMENTS
   916	// usage-limit failover) contains a verbatim signature mid-log. A healthy exit-0
   917	// APPROVED writer must not be routed into failover because of a signature in the
   918	// body it wrote. Death banners sit at the END of a log; a body hunk must not
   919	// classify. Seam: the call site classifies a bounded tail (~4 KB) — pin the
   920	// behavior, not the constant (the signature is placed FAR from the end).
   921	describe('pipeline — D1: classification uses the transcript tail, not the body', () => {
   922	  // A signature buried in the body, then >>4 KB of clean output, then a clean
   923	  // APPROVED verdict tail — the real shape of a child that wrote about limits.
   924	  const bodySignatureLog = (signature) =>
   925	    `writing README.md…\n${signature}\n` +
   926	    `diff --git a/README.md b/README.md\n` +
   927	    '+ a clean line of the documented diff hunk\n'.repeat(600) + // ~24 KB of body
   928	    `\ntokens used: 512\nDone — committed on the branch.\nVERDICT: APPROVED\nFINDINGS: none\n`;
   929	
   930	  it('RED (D1): a writer whose LOG BODY carries a usage-limit signature but whose TAIL is a clean exit-0 APPROVED is treated OK (proceeds to review, NO failover)', async () => {
   931	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
   932	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   933	      // worker-a is codex/wa-model → a codex usage-limit signature in the body.
   934	      { verdict: 'APPROVED', exitCode: 0, logContent: bodySignatureLog(CODEX_LIMIT) },
   935	      // reviewer + merger fall through to the default APPROVED.
   936	    ]);
   937	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   938	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   939	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the subtask completes — the body signature was NOT treated as a death; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   940	    assert.equal(writersOf(io.__runner).length, 1, 'the writer ran exactly once — no failover relaunch on its own work product');
   941	    assert.ok(reviewersOf(io.__runner).length >= 1, 'the run proceeded to review (the writer was classified OK)');
   942	  });
   943	
   944	  it('GUARD (D1): a death banner at the END of the log still classifies and routes to failover', async () => {
   945	    // The real death shape: clean body, banner in the TAIL. Model-unavailable
   946	    // (codex) re-resolves the next chain entry with no bundle needed.
   947	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['codex/wa1@xhigh', 'codex/wa2@xhigh'] } };
   948	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), config, runner: undefined });
   949	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   950	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: `did some work…\nall clean\n${MODEL_UNAVAIL}\n` }, // banner at the tail
   951	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // relaunched writer (wa2)
   952	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // reviewer
   953	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // merger
   954	    ]);
   955	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   956	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   957	    const relaunch = io.__runner.calls.find((c) => c.command === 'codex' && argsOf(c).includes('wa2'));
   958	    assert.ok(relaunch, 'a tail banner is still classified — the writer relaunched on the next chain entry (wa2)');
   959	  });
   960	});
   961	
   962	// ===========================================================================
   963	// D4 (dogfood milestone C) — a plain BLOCKED writer retry must not re-resolve
   964	// from the ORIGINAL chain, ignoring entries already avoided by failover.
   965	// subtaskAvoidEntries must feed EVERY writer resolution for the subtask, not
   966	// only runFailover. Live: after sol→gpt-5.5 relaunch, a BLOCKED retry reset the
   967	// writer to resolveOne('worker-a') = sol again (the avoided, dead entry).
   968	describe('pipeline — D4: avoided entries carry into a plain BLOCKED writer retry', () => {
   969	  const writerModels = (runner) => writersOf(runner).map((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '--model'));
   970	
   971	  it('RED (D4): after a model-unavailable relaunch onto wa2, a later BLOCKED retry re-runs wa2 (NOT the avoided wa1) — exactly one wa1 attempt', async () => {
   972	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['codex/wa1@xhigh', 'codex/wa2@xhigh'] } };
   973	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), config, runner: undefined });
   974	    // wa1 ALWAYS dies model-unavailable; wa2 self-check-BLOCKs once, then APPROVES.
   975	    // reviewer (sr-model) and merger (mg-model) approve.
   976	    const wa2Queue = [{ verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 0 }, { verdict: 'APPROVED', exitCode: 0 }];
   977	    const calls = [];
   978	    const runner = (spec, opts) => {
   979	      const args = (spec?.args ?? []).map(String);
   980	      const model = args[args.indexOf('--model') + 1];
   981	      calls.push({ spec, command: spec?.command, args, model });
   982	      let r;
   983	      if (model === 'wa1') {
   984	        io.fs.mkdirSync(opts.logPath.slice(0, opts.logPath.lastIndexOf('/')), { recursive: true });
   985	        io.fs.writeFileSync(opts.logPath, `working…\n${MODEL_UNAVAIL}\n`);
   986	        r = { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1 };
   987	      } else if (model === 'wa2') {
   988	        r = wa2Queue.shift() ?? { verdict: 'APPROVED', exitCode: 0 };
   989	      } else {
   990	        r = { verdict: 'APPROVED', exitCode: 0 };
   991	      }
   992	      return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict, findings: '', logPath: opts.logPath });
   993	    };
   994	    runner.calls = calls;
   995	    io.superviseChild = runner;
   996	    io.__runner = runner;
   997	
   998	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   999	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the subtask completes on wa2 after the BLOCKED retry; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1000	    const models = writerModels(runner);
  1001	    assert.equal(models.filter((m) => m === 'wa1').length, 1, `wa1 (avoided/dead) is attempted exactly once — the BLOCKED retry must re-pick wa2, not the original chain head; got ${JSON.stringify(models)}`);
  1002	    assert.ok(models.filter((m) => m === 'wa2').length >= 2, 'wa2 ran the BLOCKED attempt AND the passing retry');
  1003	  });
  1004	});
  1005	
  1006	// ===========================================================================
  1007	// D5 (dogfood milestone C) — the sessionHint passed to failover/checkpoint is
  1008	// the runId itself, with NO double 'loop-' prefix. Live artifact:
  1009	// 'loop-loop-6a96bf7b9ce6' (runId is already 'loop-<hex>').
  1010	describe('pipeline — D5: failover sessionHint is the runId (no double loop- prefix)', () => {
  1011	  it('RED (D5): after a usage-limit failover the received bundle is owned by the runId, not loop-<runId>', async () => {
  1012	    const io = makePipeRepo({
  1013	      spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }),
  1014	      files: { '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': JSON.stringify(ownedBundle('claude-code', 'open'), null, 2) + '\n' },
  1015	      config: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['claude-code/cc-w', 'codex/cx-w@xhigh'] } },
  1016	      runner: undefined,
  1017	    });
  1018	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
  1019	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: `implementing…\n${CC_LIMIT}\n` }, // writer usage-limit → seal/receive
  1020	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // relaunched writer (cx-w)
  1021	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // reviewer
  1022	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // merger
  1023	    ]);
  1024	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1025	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1026	    const runId = (await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.runId;
  1027	    assert.match(runId, /^loop-/, 'sanity: the runId is already loop-prefixed');
  1028	    const bundle = JSON.parse(io.files()['/repo/.handoff/bundle.json']);
  1029	    assert.equal(bundle.origin.sessionHint, runId, 'the failover/checkpoint session hint is the runId itself');
  1030	    assert.doesNotMatch(String(bundle.origin.sessionHint), /^loop-loop-/, 'no double loop- prefix (the live loop-loop-<hex> bug)');
  1031	  });
  1032	});
  1033	
  1034	// ===========================================================================
  1035	// D6 (dogfood milestone C, attempt 2) — reviewer and merger children get the
  1036	// SAME classify→failover treatment as writers. Live: the subtask-reviewer
  1037	// inherits the other seat's worker chain whose head was a dead model; each
  1038	// unparseable death counted as a BLOCKED review and burned the whole gate cap.
  1039	// A failure-class death must re-resolve the role (model-unavailable → entry
  1040	// avoid), NOT consume a gate iteration, and park on chain exhaustion.
  1041	describe('pipeline — D6: reviewer & merger get classify→failover like writers', () => {
  1042	  const MU_LOG = `working…\n${MODEL_UNAVAIL}\n`;
  1043	  const approve = () => ({ verdict: 'APPROVED', exitCode: 0 });
  1044	  const deathMU = () => ({ verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: MU_LOG });
  1045	  // A child runner keyed on the spawn's --model. handlers[model] is a function
  1046	  // called per spawn (closures model queues); the default is APPROVED. Throws
  1047	  // past `bound` so an unbounded re-resolution loop terminates the test RED.
  1048	  function byModelRunner(io, handlers, { bound = 40 } = {}) {
  1049	    const calls = [];
  1050	    const fn = (spec, opts) => {
  1051	      const args = (spec?.args ?? []).map(String);
  1052	      const model = args[args.indexOf('--model') + 1];
  1053	      calls.push({ spec, command: spec?.command, args, model });
  1054	      if (calls.length > bound) throw new Error(`unbounded failover: exceeded ${bound} spawns`);
  1055	      const h = handlers[model] ?? approve;
  1056	      const r = h();
  1057	      if (typeof r.logContent === 'string' && opts.logPath) {
  1058	        io.fs.mkdirSync(opts.logPath.slice(0, opts.logPath.lastIndexOf('/')), { recursive: true });
  1059	        io.fs.writeFileSync(opts.logPath, r.logContent);
  1060	      }
  1061	      return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath: opts.logPath });
  1062	    };
  1063	    fn.calls = calls;
  1064	    return fn;
  1065	  }
  1066	  const reviewerModels = (runner) => reviewersOf(runner).map((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '--model'));
  1067	  const mergersOf = (runner) => runner.calls.filter((c) => argsOf(c).join(' ').includes('You are the merger'));
  1068	  const mergerModels = (runner) => mergersOf(runner).map((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '--model'));
  1069	  const MERGES = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/merges.ndjson`;
  1070	  const mergeReceipts = (io) => {
  1071	    const raw = io.files()[MERGES];
  1072	    return typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l)) : [];
  1073	  };
  1074	  // Transitions are journaled — a re-resolved death must leave NO gate-iteration
  1075	  // event, not merely a snapshot counter that happens to read 0.
  1076	  const gateIterationEvents = (io, gate) => {
  1077	    const raw = io.files()[loopPaths('/repo').journal];
  1078	    const events = typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l)) : [];
  1079	    return events.filter((e) => e.type === 'gate-iteration' && e.gate === gate);
  1080	  };
  1081	
  1082	  it('RED (D6a): a model-unavailable subtask-reviewer re-resolves the next chain entry (one r1 then r2), no gate burn, run DONE', async () => {
  1083	    // subtask t1 uses seat a; the reviewer draws the OTHER seat's chain (worker-b).
  1084	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['codex/wa-model@xhigh'], 'worker-b': ['codex/r1@xhigh', 'codex/r2@xhigh'] } };
  1085	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), config, runner: undefined });
  1086	    const runner = byModelRunner(io, { 'wa-model': approve, r1: deathMU, r2: approve, 'mg-model': approve });
  1087	    io.superviseChild = runner;
  1088	    io.__runner = runner;
  1089	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1090	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the review succeeds via re-resolution; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1091	    assert.deepEqual(reviewerModels(runner), ['r1', 'r2'], `the dead reviewer entry re-resolved to the next; got ${JSON.stringify(reviewerModels(runner))}`);
  1092	    const state = (await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state;
  1093	    assert.equal(state.iterations['subtask-t1-review'] ?? 0, 0, 'a model-unavailable reviewer death did NOT burn a gate iteration');
  1094	    assert.equal(gateIterationEvents(io, 'subtask-t1-review').length, 0, 'NO gate-iteration event was journaled for the reviewer death');
  1095	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the run completed');
  1096	  });
  1097	
  1098	  it('RED (D6b): a model-unavailable merger re-resolves the next chain entry (one m1 then m2), the merge lands, run DONE', async () => {
  1099	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, merger: ['codex/m1@xhigh', 'codex/m2@xhigh'] } };
  1100	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), config, runner: undefined });
  1101	    const runner = byModelRunner(io, { 'wa-model': approve, 'wb-model': approve, m1: deathMU, m2: approve });
  1102	    io.superviseChild = runner;
  1103	    io.__runner = runner;
  1104	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1105	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the merge-check succeeds via re-resolution; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1106	    assert.deepEqual(mergerModels(runner), ['m1', 'm2'], `the dead merger entry re-resolved to the next; got ${JSON.stringify(mergerModels(runner))}`);
  1107	    assert.ok(mergeReceipts(io).some((r) => r.subtaskId === 't1'), 'the subtask actually merged (a receipt was written)');
  1108	    const state = (await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state;
  1109	    assert.equal(state.iterations['subtask-t1-review'] ?? 0, 0, 'a model-unavailable merger death did NOT burn a gate iteration');
  1110	    assert.equal(gateIterationEvents(io, 'subtask-t1-review').length, 0, 'NO gate-iteration event was journaled for the merger death');
  1111	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the run completed');
  1112	  });
  1113	
  1114	  it('GUARD (D6c): a genuine BLOCKED reviewer verdict STILL consumes a gate iteration (retry-with-findings unchanged)', async () => {
  1115	    // A clean exit-0 reviewer that BLOCKS on merits (no death signature), then approves.
  1116	    const reviewQ = [{ verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 0, findings: 'real review finding' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED', exitCode: 0 }];
  1117	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
  1118	    const runner = byModelRunner(io, { 'wa-model': approve, 'wb-model': () => reviewQ.shift() ?? approve(), 'mg-model': approve });
  1119	    io.superviseChild = runner;
  1120	    io.__runner = runner;
  1121	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1122	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the subtask completes after the writer addresses the review; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1123	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.iterations['subtask-t1-review'], 1, 'a genuine BLOCKED review consumes exactly one gate iteration');
  1124	  });
  1125	
  1126	  it('RED (D6d): a reviewer chain that is FULLY model-unavailable PARKS (bounded), not a cap-burn escalation', async () => {
  1127	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['codex/wa-model@xhigh'], 'worker-b': ['codex/r1@xhigh', 'codex/r2@xhigh'] } };
  1128	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }], budgets: { iterationCap: 3, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 } }), config, runner: undefined });
  1129	    const runner = byModelRunner(io, { 'wa-model': approve, r1: deathMU, r2: deathMU, 'mg-model': approve }, { bound: 16 });
  1130	    io.superviseChild = runner;
  1131	    io.__runner = runner;
  1132	    let code = 'ran';
  1133	    try { code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io); } catch { code = 'unbounded'; }
  1134	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'reviewer chain exhaustion PARKS (exit 4), never a cap-burn escalation');
  1135	    assert.ok(reviewerModels(runner).length <= 4, `reviewer re-resolution is bounded (one pass over the chain), not per-iteration re-run; got ${JSON.stringify(reviewerModels(runner))}`);
  1136	  });
  1137	});
  1138	
  1139	// ===========================================================================
  1140	// D7 (dogfood milestone C, attempt 2) — the pipeline's escalation must PERSIST.
  1141	// Live: the synthesized state spec drops budgets, so the reducer caps at its
  1142	// default (5) while drivePipeline enforces the spec cap — the run exited 3 with
  1143	// state.json still status 'running', escalation null.
  1144	describe('pipeline — D7: escalation persists to state.json', () => {
  1145	  it('RED (D7): hitting the enforced cap exits 3 AND persists status escalated naming the gate', async () => {
  1146	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];
  1147	    const gate = 'subtask-t1-review';
  1148	    const io = makePipeRepo({
  1149	      spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks, budgets: { iterationCap: 2, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 } }),
  1150	      runner: undefined,
  1151	    });
  1152	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1153	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1154	    // Seed the gate AT the enforced cap (2) — the top-of-loop cap check fires
  1155	    // before any child spawns.
  1156	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 1, status: 'running', iterations: { [gate]: 2 } });
  1157	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1158	    assert.equal(code, 3, 'a capped gate escalates (exit 3)');
  1159	    // The RAW snapshot must itself be escalated — loadLoopState replays the
  1160	    // journal over the snapshot, which could MASK a state.json left 'running'.
  1161	    const rawState = JSON.parse(io.files()[loopPaths('/repo').state]);
  1162	    assert.equal(rawState.status, 'escalated', 'the RAW state.json snapshot is escalated (not merely masked by journal replay)');
  1163	    assert.ok(rawState.escalation && rawState.escalation.gate === gate, `the raw snapshot escalation names the gate; got ${JSON.stringify(rawState.escalation)}`);
  1164	    // And the loaded view agrees.
  1165	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
  1166	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, 'the loaded state is escalated — a resume must not see a running run');
  1167	    assert.ok(state.escalation && state.escalation.gate === gate, `the loaded escalation names the gate; got ${JSON.stringify(state.escalation)}`);
  1168	  });
  1169	});
  1170	
  1171	// ===========================================================================
  1172	// D8 (dogfood milestone C, attempt 2) — an escalation with EMPTY findings still
  1173	// records WHY: a bounded tail of the last child's log lands in ESCALATION.md so
  1174	// the operator is not handed a blank escalation. The findings-present path stays
  1175	// byte-compatible.
  1176	describe('pipeline — D8: ESCALATION.md carries the last-child log tail when findings are empty', () => {
  1177	  const ESC = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/ESCALATION.md`;
  1178	
  1179	  it('RED (D8): an empty-findings escalation includes the dead child’s distinctive log tail', async () => {
  1180	    const marker = 'DISTINCTIVE_D8_TAIL_MARKER_Q9Z';
  1181	    const io = makePipeRepo({
  1182	      spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }], budgets: { iterationCap: 1, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 } }),
  1183	      runner: undefined,
  1184	    });
  1185	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
  1186	      { verdict: 'APPROVED', exitCode: 0 }, // writer passes
  1187	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 0, findings: '', logContent: `review scratch…\n${marker}\n` }, // reviewer BLOCKS with EMPTY findings but a telling log
  1188	    ]);
  1189	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1190	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1191	    assert.equal(code, 3, 'the gate escalates at the cap');
  1192	    const esc = io.files()[ESC];
  1193	    assert.ok(esc, 'ESCALATION.md was written');
  1194	    assert.match(esc, new RegExp(marker), 'an empty-findings escalation includes the last child’s log tail so the operator sees WHY');
  1195	  });
  1196	
  1197	  it('GUARD (D8): when findings are present they appear in ESCALATION.md as today (findings path unchanged)', async () => {
  1198	    const finding = 'CONCRETE_FINDING_TEXT_D8';
  1199	    const io = makePipeRepo({
  1200	      spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }], budgets: { iterationCap: 1, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 } }),
  1201	      runner: undefined,
  1202	    });
  1203	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
  1204	      { verdict: 'APPROVED', exitCode: 0 }, // writer
  1205	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 0, findings: finding }, // reviewer BLOCKS with concrete findings
  1206	    ]);
  1207	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1208	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1209	    assert.equal(code, 3, 'the gate escalates at the cap');
  1210	    const esc = io.files()[ESC];
  1211	    // Byte-for-byte the current findings-present format (pipeline.mjs:408) — a
  1212	    // format change OR an appended log tail on THIS path fails the guard.
  1213	    const expected = `# Pipeline escalation\n\nGate 'subtask-t1-review' exhausted its 1-iteration cap.\nLast findings:\n\n${finding}\n`;
  1214	    assert.equal(esc, expected, 'the findings-present ESCALATION.md is byte-identical to today (no appended tail when findings exist)');
  1215	  });
  1216	});
  1217	
  1218	// ===========================================================================
  1219	// ITEM 4 (v1.1) — findings persistence (pipeline half). A re-invoke re-prompts
  1220	// the gate's writer with the LATEST persisted findings, and a cap escalation
  1221	// embeds them even when the in-memory findings are empty (preferred over the D8
  1222	// log-tail fallback).
  1223	describe('pipeline — findings persistence (item 4)', () => {
  1224	  const FDIR = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/findings`;
  1225	  const ESC = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/ESCALATION.md`;
  1226	  // Two ordered persisted lines — the LATEST must win; the older must not be
  1227	  // selected as the active prior findings.
  1228	  const OLD = 'OLDER_FINDINGS_SUPERSEDED_PIPE';
  1229	  const NEW = 'NEWER_FINDINGS_LATEST_PIPE';
  1230	  const seedTwoFindings = (io, gate) => {
  1231	    io.fs.mkdirSync(FDIR, { recursive: true });
  1232	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${FDIR}/${gate}.ndjson`,
  1233	      JSON.stringify({ iteration: 1, findings: OLD, at: T0 }) + '\n' + JSON.stringify({ iteration: 2, findings: NEW, at: T0 }) + '\n');
  1234	  };
  1235	
  1236	  it('RED (4b pipeline): a re-invoke embeds the LATEST persisted findings in the FIRST writer prompt (older superseded)', async () => {
  1237	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];
  1238	    const gate = 'subtask-t1-review';
  1239	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), runner: undefined });
  1240	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1241	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1242	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 1, status: 'running', iterations: { [gate]: 2 } });
  1243	    seedTwoFindings(io, gate);
  1244	
  1245	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1246	    const writer = writersOf(io.__runner)[0];
  1247	    assert.ok(writer, 'a writer spawned on re-invoke');
  1248	    const prompt = argsOf(writer).join(' ');
  1249	    assert.match(prompt, new RegExp(NEW), 'the first writer prompt embeds the LATEST persisted findings (not an empty string)');
  1250	    assert.doesNotMatch(prompt, new RegExp(OLD), 'the superseded (older) findings is not selected as the active prior findings');
  1251	  });
  1252	
  1253	  it('RED (4c pipeline): a fresh-invoke cap escalation embeds the LATEST PERSISTED findings, not the D8 log tail', async () => {
  1254	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];
  1255	    const gate = 'subtask-t1-review';
  1256	    const io = makePipeRepo({
  1257	      spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks, budgets: { iterationCap: 1, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 } }),
  1258	      runner: undefined,
  1259	    });
  1260	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask()); // unused — escalates before spawn
  1261	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1262	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 1, status: 'running', iterations: { [gate]: 1 } });
  1263	    seedTwoFindings(io, gate);
  1264	
  1265	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1266	    assert.equal(code, 3, 'a gate at the cap escalates');
  1267	    const esc = io.files()[ESC];
  1268	    assert.match(esc, new RegExp(NEW), 'the cap escalation embeds the LATEST persisted findings even when in-memory findings are empty');
  1269	    assert.doesNotMatch(esc, new RegExp(OLD), 'the superseded (older) findings is not the one embedded');
  1270	    // Persisted genuinely WINS over the D8 fallback — no log-tail footer when
  1271	    // persisted findings exist.
  1272	    assert.doesNotMatch(esc, /Last child log tail|produced no parseable findings/, 'the D8 empty-findings log-tail footer is absent when persisted findings exist');
  1273	  });
  1274	});
  1275	
  1276	// ===========================================================================
  1277	// ITEM 4b (v1.1) — review artifacts, pipeline half. Multi-child gates encode the
  1278	// child ROLE in the filename: reviews/<runId>/subtask-<id>-review/iteration-NN/
  1279	// <role>.prompt.md + <role>.verdict.md, role ∈ writer|reviewer|merger (the
  1280	// writer self-check verdict included). NN = the gate iteration count at the
  1281	// WRITER spawn of the cycle (01-based) — all three children share it.
  1282	describe('pipeline — review artifacts (item 4b)', () => {
  1283	  const promptOf = (call) => (argsOf(call) ?? []).find((a) => a.includes('You are the')) ?? '';
  1284	  const parseVerdictMd = (text) => {
  1285	    const lines = String(text ?? '').split('\n');
  1286	    const header = /** @type {Record<string,string>} */ ({});
  1287	    let i = 0;
  1288	    for (; i < lines.length; i += 1) {
  1289	      const m = lines[i].match(/^([A-Za-z][\w-]*):\s?(.*)$/);
  1290	      if (!m) break;
  1291	      header[m[1].toLowerCase()] = m[2].trim();
  1292	    }
  1293	    return { header, body: lines.slice(i).join('\n').trim() };
  1294	  };
  1295	
  1296	  it('RED (4b pipeline): a clean subtask cycle writes writer/reviewer/merger prompt+verdict artifacts under iteration-01', async () => {
  1297	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];
  1298	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), runner: undefined });
  1299	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask()); // writer, reviewer, merger — all APPROVED
  1300	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1301	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1302	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the subtask completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1303	    const runId = (await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.runId;
  1304	    const base = `/repo/reviews/${runId}/subtask-t1-review/iteration-01`;
  1305	    const f = (rel) => io.files()[`${base}/${rel}`];
  1306	
  1307	    const writerCall = writersOf(io.__runner)[0];
  1308	    const reviewerCall = reviewersOf(io.__runner)[0];
  1309	    const mergerCall = io.__runner.calls.find((c) => argsOf(c).join(' ').includes('You are the merger'));
  1310	    for (const [role, call, model] of [['writer', writerCall, 'wa-model'], ['reviewer', reviewerCall, 'wb-model'], ['merger', mergerCall, 'mg-model']]) {
  1311	      assert.ok(call, `${role} child ran`);
  1312	      assert.ok(f(`${role}.prompt.md`), `${role}.prompt.md written under iteration-01`);
  1313	      assert.ok(f(`${role}.verdict.md`), `${role}.verdict.md written under iteration-01`);
  1314	      assert.equal(f(`${role}.prompt.md`), promptOf(call), `${role}.prompt.md === the ${role} prompt verbatim`);
  1315	      const v = parseVerdictMd(f(`${role}.verdict.md`));
  1316	      assert.equal(v.header.verdict, 'APPROVED', `${role} verdict header: exact verdict`);
  1317	      assert.match(v.header.model ?? '', new RegExp(model), `${role} verdict header: concrete model`);
  1318	      assert.match(v.header.date ?? '', /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/, `${role} verdict header: date`);
  1319	      assert.ok('degraded' in v.header, `${role} verdict header: degraded field present`);
  1320	    }
  1321	  });
  1322	});
  1323	
  1324	// ===========================================================================
  1325	// ITEM 9 (v1.1) — pipeline smoke gate. After the FINAL subtask merges, a
  1326	// pipeline whose loop.json sets smoke.cmd reuses the loop's smoke machinery
  1327	// (smokeApprovalToken/verifySmokeToken, SMOKE_AWAIT/SMOKE_APPROVE, --approve-smoke):
  1328	// run smoke.cmd, await a drift-bound token, complete only on a verified token.
  1329	describe('pipeline — smoke gate (item 9)', () => {
  1330	  const DIR = loopPaths('/repo').dir;
  1331	  // A git that also answers the smoke command (non-git → 'ok'), so merges work
  1332	  // AND the smoke.cmd runs through io.execFile.
  1333	  const smokeAwareIo = (spec, { git = pipelineGit() } = {}) => {
  1334	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec, git, runner: undefined });
  1335	    const baseExec = io.execFile;
  1336	    io.execFile = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = [], /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) =>
  1337	      cmd === 'git' ? baseExec(cmd, args, opts) : Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'ok\n', stderr: '' });
  1338	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1339	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1340	    return io;
  1341	  };
  1342	  const smokeSpec = () => pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }], smoke: { cmd: 'npm run smoke', expect: 'ok' } });
  1343	
  1344	  it('RED (9-1): a pipeline with smoke.cmd AWAITS approval after the last subtask merges (not straight to DONE)', async () => {
  1345	    const io = smokeAwareIo(smokeSpec());
  1346	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1347	    assert.equal(code, 0, `awaiting smoke approval is a clean exit 0; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1348	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL, 'the run awaits smoke approval, not DONE');
  1349	    assert.ok(io.files()[`${DIR}/merges.ndjson`]?.includes('t1'), 'the subtask merged BEFORE the smoke gate');
  1350	    assert.ok(io.files()[`${DIR}/SMOKE-REVIEW.md`], 'SMOKE-REVIEW.md was written');
  1351	    const approval = io.files()[`${DIR}/smoke-approval.json`];
  1352	    assert.ok(approval, 'smoke-approval.json was written');
  1353	    const token = JSON.parse(approval).token;
  1354	    assert.ok(typeof token === 'string' && token.startsWith('smk1.'), 'a drift-bound smk token is issued');
  1355	  });
  1356	
  1357	  it('RED (9-2): pipeline run --approve-smoke <token> verifies and completes the run DONE', async () => {
  1358	    const io = smokeAwareIo(smokeSpec());
  1359	    assert.equal(await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io), 0);
  1360	    const approval = io.files()[`${DIR}/smoke-approval.json`];
  1361	    assert.ok(approval, 'run 1 reached the smoke gate and issued a token');
  1362	    const token = JSON.parse(approval).token;
  1363	
  1364	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run', '--approve-smoke', token], io);
  1365	    assert.equal(code, 0, `an approved pipeline completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1366	    const raw = JSON.parse(io.files()[loopPaths('/repo').state]);
  1367	    assert.equal(raw.status, 'done', 'the approved run persists status done');
  1368	  });
  1369	
  1370	  it('RED (9-3): a DRIFTED smoke token is refused and the pipeline PARKS (exit 4)', async () => {
  1371	    const git = pipelineGit();
  1372	    const io = smokeAwareIo(smokeSpec(), { git });
  1373	    assert.equal(await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io), 0);
  1374	    const approval = io.files()[`${DIR}/smoke-approval.json`];
  1375	    assert.ok(approval, 'run 1 issued a token');
  1376	    const token = JSON.parse(approval).token;
  1377	    // A bound input drifts: main moves after the human looked.
  1378	    git.moveMain('DRIFTED-SHA-9-3-AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA');
  1379	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run', '--approve-smoke', token], io);
  1380	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a drifted token parks the pipeline (exit 4)');
  1381	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'drift parks the run');
  1382	  });
  1383	
  1384	  it('GUARD (9-4): a pipeline with NO smoke.cmd completes straight to DONE (no regression)', async () => {
  1385	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
  1386	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1387	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1388	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1389	    assert.equal(code, 0);
  1390	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'no smoke.cmd → straight to DONE');
  1391	    assert.ok(!io.files()[`${DIR}/smoke-approval.json`], 'no smoke gate is armed when smoke.cmd is unset');
  1392	  });
  1393	
  1394	  it('GUARD (9-5): a pipeline spec with smoke.cmd and NO phases is ACCEPTED — the loop smoke-build rule never gates pipelines', async () => {
  1395	    const spec = smokeSpec();
  1396	    assert.ok(!('phases' in spec), 'precondition: the pipeline spec has subtasks, no phases');
  1397	    const io = smokeAwareIo(spec);
  1398	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1399	    assert.notEqual(code, 2, 'smoke.cmd + no phases is NOT a validation error for a pipeline (no smoke-build refusal)');
  1400	    assert.ok(io.__runner.calls.length >= 1, 'it ran past validation and spawned the subtask');
  1401	  });
  1402	});

  1405	// ITEM 10 (v1.1) — --detach (pipeline half). Mirrors the loop detach contract
  1406	// through the same injected `io.spawnDetached` seam (see loop-run.test.mjs for
  1407	// the full seam rationale). POSIX-only — SKIP_WIN.
  1408	describe('pipeline — --detach (item 10)', () => {
  1409	  const SKIP_WIN = process.platform === 'win32';
  1410	  const DIR = loopPaths('/repo').dir;
  1411	
  1412	  it('RED (10-1 pipeline): pipeline run --detach forks a detached supervisor; parent exits 0, lock names the detached pid, stdout prints pid + tail hint', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
  1413	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
  1414	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1415	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1416	    const DETACHED_PID = 515151;
  1417	    const calls = [];
  1418	    io.spawnDetached = (/** @type {any} */ spec) => {
  1419	      calls.push(spec);
  1420	      io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
  1421	      io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: io.host, pid: DETACHED_PID, startTime: 1, runId: `sup-${DETACHED_PID}` }));
  1422	      return { pid: DETACHED_PID };
  1423	    };
  1424	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run', '--detach'], io);
  1425	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the parent returns promptly with exit 0; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1426	    assert.equal(calls.length, 1, 'the supervisor was forked via the detach seam exactly once');
  1427	    assert.equal(JSON.parse(io.files()[`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`]).pid, DETACHED_PID, 'the run lock names the detached supervisor pid');
  1428	    assert.match(io.stdoutText(), new RegExp(String(DETACHED_PID)), 'stdout prints the detached pid');
  1429	    assert.match(io.stdoutText(), /supervisor\.out/, 'stdout prints a tail hint for .handoff/loop/supervisor.out');
  1430	  });
  1431	});

  1433	// ===========================================================================
  1434	// ITEM 8 (v1.1) — probe integration (pipeline half). readProbeCache is loaded
  1435	// once and passed as probes: into resolveOne (writer/reviewer/merger) AND every
  1436	// runFailover. A fresh rate-limited platform diverts the first spawn and every
  1437	// re-resolution; stale/missing cache is null (today's behavior).
  1438	describe('pipeline — probe integration (item 8)', () => {
  1439	  const CACHE = '/repo/.handoff/log/probe-cache.json';
  1440	  const seedCache = (io, records, at = T0) => {
  1441	    io.fs.mkdirSync('/repo/.handoff/log', { recursive: true });
  1442	    io.fs.writeFileSync(CACHE, JSON.stringify({ at, records }));
  1443	  };
  1444	  const rateLimited = (platform) => [{ platform, capability: 'installed', outcome: 'rate-limited' }];
  1445	  // Identify children by their prompt ROLE phrase — robust across platforms
  1446	  // (claude writers carry no `-s workspace-write` flag, so writersOf misses them).
  1447	  const writerChildren = (io) => io.__runner.calls.filter((c) => argsOf(c).join(' ').includes('You are the writer'));
  1448	  const reviewerChildren = (io) => io.__runner.calls.filter((c) => argsOf(c).join(' ').includes('You are the subtask-reviewer'));
  1449	
  1450	  it('RED (8-2): a fresh cache rate-limiting worker-a head diverts the FIRST writer to the fallback', async () => {
  1451	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['claude-code/cc-wa', 'codex/cx-wa@xhigh'] } };
  1452	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), config, runner: undefined });
  1453	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1454	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1455	    seedCache(io, rateLimited('claude-code'));
  1456	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1457	    const writer = writerChildren(io)[0];
  1458	    assert.ok(writer, 'a writer spawned');
  1459	    assert.notEqual(writer.command, 'claude', 'the rate-limited claude-code never spawned as the writer');
  1460	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writer), '--model'), 'cx-wa', 'the first writer diverted to the codex worker-a fallback');
  1461	  });
  1462	
  1463	  it('RED (8-2 failover): a failover relaunch honors the cache — the rate-limited middle entry is skipped for the surviving one', async () => {
  1464	    // worker-a: codex head (dies model-unavailable) → claude-code middle (RATE-LIMITED
  1465	    // by the cache) → cursor tail. The relaunch must skip the middle and land on cursor.
  1466	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['codex/cx-head@xhigh', 'claude-code/cc-mid', 'cursor/cu-tail'] } };
  1467	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), config, runner: undefined });
  1468	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
  1469	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: `working…\n${MODEL_UNAVAIL}\n` }, // cx-head dies model-unavailable
  1470	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // the relaunched writer
  1471	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // reviewer
  1472	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // merger
  1473	    ]);
  1474	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1475	    seedCache(io, rateLimited('claude-code'));
  1476	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1477	    const relaunch = writerChildren(io)[1];
  1478	    assert.ok(relaunch, 'a relaunched writer spawned after the model-unavailable death');
  1479	    assert.notEqual(relaunch.command, 'claude', 'the failover skipped the rate-limited claude-code middle entry');
  1480	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(relaunch), '--model'), 'cu-tail', 'the failover relaunch landed on the surviving cursor entry (cache honored in runFailover)');
  1481	  });
  1482	
  1483	  it('RED (8-3): the reviewer re-resolution honors the cache — a rate-limited head diverts the reviewer', async () => {
  1484	    // Subtask t1 reviews from the OTHER seat (worker-b); rate-limit its head.
  1485	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-b': ['claude-code/cc-rev', 'codex/cx-rev@xhigh'] } };
  1486	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), config, runner: undefined });
  1487	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1488	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1489	    seedCache(io, rateLimited('claude-code'));
  1490	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1491	    const reviewer = reviewerChildren(io)[0];
  1492	    assert.ok(reviewer, 'a reviewer spawned');
  1493	    assert.notEqual(reviewer.command, 'claude', 'the rate-limited claude-code never spawned as the reviewer');
  1494	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(reviewer), '--model'), 'cx-rev', 'the reviewer diverted to the codex worker-b fallback');
  1495	  });
  1496	
  1497	  const mergerChildren = (io) => io.__runner.calls.filter((c) => argsOf(c).join(' ').includes('You are the merger'));
  1498	
  1499	  it('RED (8-3 reviewer failover): a reviewer failover relaunch honors the cache — dead head avoided, rate-limited middle skipped for the surviving tail', async () => {
  1500	    // worker-b (the reviewer chain): codex head dies model-unavailable → claude-code
  1501	    // middle (RATE-LIMITED) → cursor tail. The relaunch must land on cursor.
  1502	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-b': ['codex/cx-rvh@xhigh', 'claude-code/cc-rvm', 'cursor/cu-rvt'] } };
  1503	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), config, runner: undefined });
  1504	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
  1505	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // writer (worker-a, default codex)
  1506	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: `reviewing…\n${MODEL_UNAVAIL}\n` }, // reviewer head dies
  1507	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // relaunched reviewer
  1508	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // merger
  1509	    ]);
  1510	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1511	    seedCache(io, rateLimited('claude-code'));
  1512	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1513	    const relaunch = reviewerChildren(io)[1];
  1514	    assert.ok(relaunch, 'a relaunched reviewer spawned after the model-unavailable death');
  1515	    assert.notEqual(relaunch.command, 'claude', 'the reviewer failover skipped the rate-limited claude-code middle entry');
  1516	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(relaunch), '--model'), 'cu-rvt', 'the reviewer relaunch landed on the surviving cursor tail (cache reached superviseVerdictChild runFailover)');
  1517	  });
  1518	
  1519	  it('RED (8-3 merger failover): a merger failover relaunch honors the cache — dead head avoided, rate-limited middle skipped for the surviving tail', async () => {
  1520	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, merger: ['codex/cx-mgh@xhigh', 'claude-code/cc-mgm', 'cursor/cu-mgt'] } };
  1521	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), config, runner: undefined });
  1522	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
  1523	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // writer
  1524	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // reviewer
  1525	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: `merge-check…\n${MODEL_UNAVAIL}\n` }, // merger head dies
  1526	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // relaunched merger
  1527	    ]);
  1528	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1529	    seedCache(io, rateLimited('claude-code'));
  1530	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1531	    const relaunch = mergerChildren(io)[1];
  1532	    assert.ok(relaunch, 'a relaunched merger spawned after the model-unavailable death');
  1533	    assert.notEqual(relaunch.command, 'claude', 'the merger failover skipped the rate-limited claude-code middle entry');
  1534	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(relaunch), '--model'), 'cu-mgt', 'the merger relaunch landed on the surviving cursor tail (cache reached superviseVerdictChild runFailover)');
  1535	  });
  1536	
  1537	  it('GUARD (8-4 pipeline): a STALE cache and a MISSING cache both change nothing — the writer head spawns as today', async () => {
  1538	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['claude-code/cc-wa', 'codex/cx-wa@xhigh'] } };
  1539	    const staleAt = new Date(Date.parse(T0) - 20 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
  1540	
  1541	    const ioStale = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), config, runner: undefined });
  1542	    ioStale.superviseChild = fakeRunner(ioStale, cleanSubtask());
  1543	    ioStale.__runner = ioStale.superviseChild;
  1544	    seedCache(ioStale, rateLimited('claude-code'), staleAt);
  1545	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], ioStale);
  1546	    assert.equal(writerChildren(ioStale)[0].command, 'claude', 'a stale cache is ignored — the writer head (claude-code) spawns as today');
  1547	
  1548	    const ioNone = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), config, runner: undefined });
  1549	    ioNone.superviseChild = fakeRunner(ioNone, cleanSubtask());
  1550	    ioNone.__runner = ioNone.superviseChild;
  1551	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], ioNone);
  1552	    assert.equal(writerChildren(ioNone)[0].command, 'claude', 'no cache file — the writer head spawns as today');
  1553	  });
  1554	
  1555	  it('GUARD (8-5 pipeline): an unverifiable-not-rate-limited head still resolves (never blocked) and its verdict.md keeps the degraded audit field', async () => {
  1556	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['claude-code/cc-wa', 'codex/cx-wa@xhigh'] } };
  1557	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), config, runner: undefined });
  1558	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1559	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1560	    // installed/ok = auth unverified but NOT rate-limited → selectable (degraded).
  1561	    seedCache(io, [{ platform: 'claude-code', capability: 'installed', outcome: 'ok' }]);
  1562	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1563	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'an unverifiable head is not blocked — the run completes');
  1564	    const writer = writerChildren(io)[0];
  1565	    assert.equal(writer.command, 'claude', 'installed/ok is selectable — the head still spawns, never skipped');
  1566	    // The degraded audit field is preserved in the gate artifact header.
  1567	    const runId = (await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.runId;
  1568	    const verdictMd = io.files()[`/repo/reviews/${runId}/subtask-t1-review/iteration-01/writer.verdict.md`];
  1569	    assert.ok(verdictMd, 'the writer verdict.md artifact exists');
  1570	    assert.match(verdictMd, /^degraded: .+$/m, 'the degraded audit field is present and non-empty');

  1573	
  1574	// ===========================================================================
  1575	// ITEM 2 (v1.1) — trunk derivation. Replace hardcoded 'main' with the repo's
  1576	// actual default branch (git symbolic-ref refs/remotes/origin/HEAD → fallback
  1577	// rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD at setup), recorded in state and reused, never
  1578	// re-derived mid-run. Pinned in a MASTER-trunk fake repo — the observable is
  1579	// "master used everywhere; one derivation", not the exact command.
  1580	
  1581	// A fake git modeling a MASTER-trunk repo: the default branch is 'master'
  1582	// (symbolic-ref refs/remotes/origin/HEAD → origin/master; fallback rev-parse
  1583	// --abbrev-ref HEAD at the ROOT → master). It answers 'main' refs LENIENTLY
  1584	// (same sha/ranges) so a main-hardcoded impl still runs end to end — the pin's
  1585	// teeth are that NO 'main' appears in the recorded git argv. deriveThrows makes
  1586	// the default-branch derivation fail (to prove a resume reuses a stamped trunk).
  1587	// Structured argv classifiers shared by the fake's dispatch AND its call-count
  1588	// helpers, so both agree on what "a trunk-derivation probe" is (verifier iter 3):
  1589	// any arg order, plus symbolic-ref HEAD and branch --show-current variants.
  1590	function isDerivationShape(args) {
  1591	  const has = (a) => args.includes(a);
  1592	  return (
  1593	    (has('symbolic-ref') && (has('refs/remotes/origin/HEAD') || has('HEAD'))) || // (a) + (d)
  1594	    (has('rev-parse') && has('--abbrev-ref') && has('HEAD')) || // (b) any order
  1595	    (has('branch') && has('--show-current')) // (c)
  1596	  );
  1597	}
  1598	// The one legit exception: rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD (any order) in a seat cwd.
  1599	function isSeatHeadProbe(args, cwd) {
  1600	  const seat = cwd === WT_A || cwd === WT_B;
  1601	  return seat && args.includes('rev-parse') && args.includes('--abbrev-ref') && args.includes('HEAD');
  1602	}
  1603	
  1604	function masterGit({ merged = [], existingBranches = [], dirtyCwds = [], log = CLEAN_LOG, deriveThrows = false } = {}) {
  1605	  const added = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map());
  1606	  const branchAt = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map());
  1607	  const branches = new Set(existingBranches);
  1608	  const dirty = new Set(dirtyCwds);
  1609	  const mergedSet = new Set(merged);
  1610	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
  1611	  const lockProbeRef = { fn: /** @type {null | (() => boolean)} */ (null) };
  1612	  const list = () => {
  1613	    const entries = [{ path: '/repo', branch: 'master' }];
  1614	    for (const [path, branch] of added) entries.push({ path, branch: branchAt.get(path) ?? branch });
  1615	    return entries.map((e) => `worktree ${e.path}\nHEAD ${MAIN_SHA}\nbranch refs/heads/${e.branch}\n`).join('\n');
  1616	  };
  1617	  const fn = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = [], /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
  1618	    const argstr = args.join(' ');
  1619	    calls.push({ cmd, args, argstr, cwd: opts?.cwd, lockPresent: lockProbeRef.fn ? lockProbeRef.fn() : undefined });
  1620	    if (cmd !== 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
  1621	    // ---- structured trunk-derivation classification (verifier iter 3) --------
  1622	    // Every current-/default-branch probe shape is classified by ARGV, not by an
  1623	    // exact string, so arg-order variants and alternate commands can't slip past:
  1624	    //   (a) symbolic-ref naming refs/remotes/origin/HEAD
  1625	    //   (b) rev-parse containing BOTH --abbrev-ref and HEAD (any order)
  1626	    //   (c) branch --show-current
  1627	    //   (d) symbolic-ref HEAD
  1628	    // The ONLY legit exception is form (b) issued in a seat cwd (the pipeline's
  1629	    // alreadyOnBranch / preflight / postflight HEAD checks).
  1630	    const seatCwd = opts?.cwd === WT_A || opts?.cwd === WT_B;
  1631	    if (isSeatHeadProbe(args, opts?.cwd)) {
  1632	      const dflt = opts.cwd === WT_A ? 'baton/wt-a/base' : 'baton/wt-b/base'; // never 'master' — a seat probe is never the trunk
  1633	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${branchAt.get(opts.cwd) ?? dflt}\n`, stderr: '' });
  1634	    }
  1635	    if (isDerivationShape(args) && !seatCwd) {
  1636	      // A derivation attempt (root fallback or a stray probe in any non-seat cwd).
  1637	      // deriveThrows makes EVERY such form fail so it can't silently succeed via a
  1638	      // generic catch-all; otherwise it returns the shape-appropriate default.
  1639	      if (deriveThrows) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('fatal: no default branch'), { code: 128, stderr: 'fatal: no default branch\n' }));
  1640	      if (args.includes('symbolic-ref')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${args.includes('refs/remotes/origin/HEAD') ? 'refs/remotes/origin/master' : 'refs/heads/master'}\n`, stderr: '' });
  1641	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'master\n', stderr: '' }); // rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD / branch --show-current
  1642	    }
  1643	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree list')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: list(), stderr: '' });
  1644	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree add')) {
  1645	      const path = args.find((a) => a.startsWith('/repo/.worktrees/'));
  1646	      const bi = args.indexOf('-b');
  1647	      const branch = bi >= 0 ? args[bi + 1] : 'baton/wt-x/base';
  1648	      if (path) { added.set(path, branch); branchAt.set(path, branch); }
  1649	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
  1650	    }
  1651	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree prune') || argstr.startsWith('worktree remove')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
  1652	    if (argstr.startsWith('checkout -b')) {
  1653	      const target = args[args.indexOf('-b') + 1];
  1654	      if (branches.has(target)) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`fatal: a branch named '${target}' already exists`), { code: 128, stderr: `fatal: a branch named '${target}' already exists\n` }));
  1655	      branches.add(target);
  1656	      if (opts?.cwd) branchAt.set(opts.cwd, target);
  1657	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
  1658	    }
  1659	    if (argstr.startsWith('status --porcelain')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: dirty.has(opts?.cwd) ? ' M dirty.txt\n' : '', stderr: '' });
  1660	    // merge-base --is-ancestor <branch> <trunk>: exit 0 iff branch already merged.
  1661	    if (argstr.startsWith('merge-base --is-ancestor')) {
  1662	      const b = args[args.indexOf('--is-ancestor') + 1];
  1663	      return mergedSet.has(b) ? Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }) : Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('not an ancestor'), { code: 1, stdout: '', stderr: '' }));
  1664	    }
  1665	    // Lenient: rev-parse of ANY single ref (master OR main) resolves to the sha.
  1666	    if (argstr.startsWith('rev-parse')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${MAIN_SHA}\n`, stderr: '' });
  1667	    if (argstr.startsWith('log ')) {
  1668	      const range = args.find((a) => /\.\./.test(String(a))) ?? '';
  1669	      const branch = String(range).split('..')[1];
  1670	      if (branch && mergedSet.has(branch)) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }); // merged → empty
  1671	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: log, stderr: '' });
  1672	    }
  1673	    if (argstr.startsWith('merge')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'Fast-forward', stderr: '' }); // merge <branch> and merge --ff-only <trunk>
  1674	    if (argstr.startsWith('branch -D')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
  1675	    return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`unstubbed git: ${argstr}`), { code: 'ENOSTUB' }));
  1676	  };
  1677	  fn.calls = calls;
  1678	  fn.git = () => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git');
  1679	  fn.mainCalls = () => fn.git().filter((c) => c.argstr.includes('main'));
  1680	  // A trunk-derivation attempt = ANY derivation SHAPE (symbolic-ref origin/HEAD,
  1681	  // rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD in any order, branch --show-current, symbolic-ref
  1682	  // HEAD) that is NOT the legit seat HEAD probe. Counts seat-cwd re-derivations of
  1683	  // other shapes too — the only exclusion is the explicit seat HEAD-probe form —
  1684	  // so "derive once, never re-derive" is pinned by the count, arg-order-proof.
  1685	  fn.derivationCalls = () => fn.git().filter((c) => isDerivationShape(c.args) && !isSeatHeadProbe(c.args, c.cwd)).length;
  1686	  // The legit seat HEAD probes (alreadyOnBranch / preflight / postflight),
  1687	  // confined to the two seat cwds — asserted explicitly.
  1688	  fn.seatProbes = () => fn.git().filter((c) => isSeatHeadProbe(c.args, c.cwd));
  1689	  fn.setLockProbe = (/** @type {() => boolean} */ f) => { lockProbeRef.fn = f; };
  1690	  return fn;
  1691	}
  1692	
  1693	describe('pipeline — trunk derivation (v1.1 item 2)', () => {
  1694	  const MERGES = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/merges.ndjson`;
  1695	  const mergeReceiptsOf = (io) => {
  1696	    const raw = io.files()[MERGES];
  1697	    return typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l)) : [];
  1698	  };
  1699	
  1700	  it('RED (trunk-1): a master-trunk pipeline completes DONE using master everywhere — no "main" in any git argv; prompts range over master', async () => {
  1701	    const git = masterGit();
  1702	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), git, runner: undefined });
  1703	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1704	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1705	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1706	    assert.equal(code, 0, `a master-trunk run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1707	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the run reached DONE');
  1708	    // Teeth: NO trunk touchpoint used the hardcoded 'main'.
  1709	    assert.deepEqual(git.mainCalls().map((c) => c.argstr), [], 'every trunk git call targets master — no main anywhere');
  1710	    // Reviewer prompt ranges over master..branch.
  1711	    const rev = reviewersOf(io.__runner)[0];
  1712	    assert.ok(rev, 'a reviewer ran');
  1713	    const revPrompt = argsOf(rev).join(' ');
  1714	    assert.match(revPrompt, /git diff master\.\.baton\/wt-a\/subtask-t1/, 'the reviewer prompt ranges over master..<branch>');
  1715	    assert.doesNotMatch(revPrompt, /\bmain\.\./, 'the reviewer prompt never ranges over main');
  1716	    // Merger prompt names master as the trunk.
  1717	    const merger = io.__runner.calls.find((c) => argsOf(c).join(' ').includes('You are the merger'));
  1718	    assert.ok(merger, 'a merger ran');
  1719	    assert.match(argsOf(merger).join(' '), /against master/, 'the merger prompt names master as the trunk');
  1720	    // Receipt shape unchanged.
  1721	    assert.ok(
  1722	      mergeReceiptsOf(io).some((r) => r.subtaskId === 't1' && r.branch === 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1' && typeof r.mergedAt === 'string'),
  1723	      'the merge receipt keeps its {subtaskId, branch, mergedAt} shape',
  1724	    );
  1725	  });
  1726	
  1727	  it('RED (trunk-2): the stale-branch park message names the trunk (git log master..<branch>), not main', async () => {
  1728	    const branch = 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1';
  1729	    const git = masterGit({ merged: [branch] }); // is-ancestor true, empty master..branch, and NO receipt → stale park
  1730	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), git, runner: undefined });
  1731	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1732	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1733	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1734	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'an ancestor-without-receipt stale branch parks');
  1735	    const out = io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText();
  1736	    assert.match(out, /git log master\.\.baton\/wt-a\/subtask-t1/, 'the stale-park message points at git log master..<branch>');
  1737	    assert.doesNotMatch(out, /\bmain\.\./, 'the stale-park message never ranges over main');
  1738	  });
  1739	
  1740	  it('RED (trunk-3a): a fresh master-trunk run STAMPS the derived trunk into state.json', async () => {
  1741	    const git = masterGit();
  1742	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), git, runner: undefined });
  1743	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1744	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1745	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1746	    const state = JSON.parse(io.files()[loopPaths('/repo').state]);
  1747	    assert.equal(state.trunk, 'master', 'the derived trunk is persisted in state.json for reuse');
  1748	    assert.equal(git.derivationCalls(), 1, 'the trunk is derived EXACTLY once at init (one root-level derivation)');
  1749	  });
  1750	
  1751	  it('RED (trunk-3b): resume REUSES the stamped trunk and does not re-derive (completes even when derivation now fails)', async () => {
  1752	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];
  1753	    const git = masterGit({ deriveThrows: true }); // deriving would fail now — the resume must not need it
  1754	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), git, runner: undefined });
  1755	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1756	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1757	    // A prior run already derived + stamped the trunk.
  1758	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 1, status: 'running', trunk: 'master' });
  1759	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1760	    assert.equal(code, 0, `resume completes using the stamped trunk without re-deriving; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1761	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the resumed run reached DONE');
  1762	    assert.deepEqual(git.mainCalls().map((c) => c.argstr), [], 'the resumed run uses the stamped master everywhere');
  1763	    // Derive-once teeth: a stamped resume issues NO derivation — not symbolic-ref,
  1764	    // not a root fallback, and not a stray abbrev-ref in any non-seat cwd (a
  1765	    // seat-cwd re-derivation would count here too, since only WT_A/WT_B probes
  1766	    // are excluded).
  1767	    assert.equal(git.derivationCalls(), 0, 'a stamped resume NEVER re-derives the trunk (not even to catch-and-fallback, and not via a seat cwd)');
  1768	    // The exclusion is explicit: the only abbrev-ref HEAD calls are the known
  1769	    // legit seat probes, confined to the two seat cwds.
  1770	    assert.ok(git.seatProbes().length >= 1, 'the resume made its legit seat HEAD probes');
  1771	    assert.ok(git.seatProbes().every((c) => c.cwd === WT_A || c.cwd === WT_B), 'every abbrev-ref HEAD probe is a seat probe (WT_A/WT_B), never a disguised derivation');
  1772	  });
  1773	
  1774	  it('RED (trunk-4): a stamped state WITHOUT a trunk field migrates — derives once and stamps it, never a refusal', async () => {
  1775	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];
  1776	    const git = masterGit();
  1777	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), git, runner: undefined });
  1778	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1779	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1780	    // A pre-trunk stamped state: flavor + specDigest present, but NO trunk field.
  1781	    // Missing trunk alone is NOT the I3 legacy-unstamped refusal.
  1782	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 1, status: 'running' });
  1783	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1784	    assert.notEqual(code, 2, 'a missing trunk field is additive migration, not a legacy-unstamped refusal');
  1785	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the run migrates and completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1786	    const state = JSON.parse(io.files()[loopPaths('/repo').state]);
  1787	    assert.equal(state.trunk, 'master', 'the trunk is derived once and stamped on migration');
  1788	    assert.equal(git.derivationCalls(), 1, 'migration derives the trunk EXACTLY once, then stamps it');
  1789	  });
  1790	
  1791	  it('RED (trunk-5): a receipt-backed already-merged subtask auto-advances under master (no t1 writer), zero main in argv', async () => {
  1792	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'first' }, { id: 't2', title: 'second' }];
  1793	    const t1Branch = 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1';
  1794	    // t1 is genuinely merged under master: is-ancestor true, empty master..t1,
  1795	    // AND a receipt. t2 is unmerged and runs normally.
  1796	    const git = masterGit({ merged: [t1Branch] });
  1797	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), git, runner: undefined });
  1798	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask()); // enough for t2 only

  1800	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 2, status: 'running', trunk: 'master' });
  1801	    io.fs.mkdirSync(loopPaths('/repo').dir, { recursive: true });
  1802	    io.fs.writeFileSync(MERGES, JSON.stringify({ subtaskId: 't1', branch: t1Branch, mergedAt: T0 }) + '\n');
  1803

  1806	    assert.doesNotMatch(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /EMPTY branch|nothing to review or merge|stale/i, 'a receipt-backed merged subtask is not mistaken for empty/stale under master');
  1807	    const writers = writersOf(io.__runner);
  1808	    assert.ok(writers.length >= 1, 'the unmerged subtask still runs a writer');
  1809	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[0]), '-C'), WT_B, 'the first writer is t2 (wt-b) — receipt-backed t1 was recognized as merged and skipped');
  1810	    assert.ok(!writers.some((c) => argsOf(c).join(' ').includes("subtask 't1'")), 'no writer ran for the already-merged t1');
  1811	    assert.deepEqual(git.mainCalls().map((c) => c.argstr), [], 'the already-merged recognizer and merge path use master — no main anywhere');
  1812	  });
  1813	});
