  1815	// ===========================================================================
  1816	// H5 (B4) — state flavor + spec binding across loop/pipeline.
  1817	describe('pipeline — H5: state flavor + spec-digest binding', () => {
  1818	  it('RED (H5a): a state.json created by `loop run` (flavor loop) is REFUSED by pipeline run (exit 2 naming the mismatch), zero spawns', async () => {
  1819	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
  1820	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1821	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1822	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'loop', phaseCount: 1 });
  1823	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1824	    assert.equal(code, 2, 'a loop-flavored state cannot be resumed as a pipeline');
  1825	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /flavor|loop|mismatch/i, 'the refusal names the flavor mismatch');
  1826	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned on a flavor mismatch');
  1827	  });
  1828	
  1829	  it('RED (H5b): a changed subtasks list (stale specDigest) on resume REFUSES (exit 2), rather than misaligning phaseIndex', async () => {
  1830	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'a' }, { id: 't2', title: 'b' }] }), runner: undefined });
  1831	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
  1832	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1833	    // A prior run over a DIFFERENT subtask list: its specDigest no longer matches
  1834	    // the current subtasks, so resuming its phaseIndex would misalign.
  1835	    const oldSubtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'a' }, { id: 'REMOVED', title: 'gone' }, { id: 't2', title: 'b' }];
  1836	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(oldSubtasks), phaseIndex: 1 });
  1837	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1838	    assert.equal(code, 2, 'a resume against a changed subtask list refuses rather than resuming a misaligned position');
  1839	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /subtask|spec|changed|digest/i, 'the refusal names the spec change');
  1840	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned on a spec-digest mismatch');
  1841	  });
  1842	});
  1843	
  1844	// ===========================================================================
  1845	// I1 (A1/B1) — auto-advance requires a supervisor-owned MERGE RECEIPT, not just
  1846	// is-ancestor: a stale empty branch at main's tip is is-ancestor-true too, and
  1847	// must PARK, not silently skip the subtask.
  1848	//
  1849	// Merge-receipt shape (I1 design): append-only `.handoff/loop/merges.ndjson`,
  1850	// one line per merged subtask `{ subtaskId, branch, mergedAt }`. Auto-advance on
  1851	// resume requires is-ancestor(branch, main) AND a receipt for that subtask.
  1852	describe('pipeline — I1: merge-receipt-gated auto-advance', () => {
  1853	  const MERGES = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/merges.ndjson`;
  1854	  const seedReceipt = (io, subtaskId, branch) => {
  1855	    io.fs.mkdirSync(loopPaths('/repo').dir, { recursive: true });
  1856	    io.fs.writeFileSync(MERGES, JSON.stringify({ subtaskId, branch, mergedAt: T0 }) + '\n');
  1857	  };
  1858	  const receipts = (io) => {
  1859	    const raw = io.files()[MERGES];
  1860	    return typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l)) : [];
  1861	  };
  1862	
  1863	  it('RED (I1b): a receipt-backed already-merged subtask ADVANCES on resume (no writer, no empty-branch park)', async () => {
  1864	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'first' }, { id: 't2', title: 'second' }];
  1865	    // t1 is genuinely merged: is-ancestor true, empty main..t1, AND a receipt.
  1866	    const git = pipelineGit({ mergedBranches: ['baton/wt-a/subtask-t1'] });
  1867	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), git, runner: undefined });
  1868	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask()); // enough for t2 only
  1869	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1870	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 2 });
  1871	    seedReceipt(io, 't1', 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1');
  1872	
  1873	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1874	    assert.equal(code, 0, `a receipt-backed merged subtask advances and the run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1875	    assert.doesNotMatch(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /EMPTY branch|nothing to review or merge|stale/i, 'a receipt-backed subtask is not mistaken for empty/stale');
  1876	    const writers = writersOf(io.__runner);
  1877	    assert.ok(writers.length >= 1, 'the unmerged subtask still runs a writer');
  1878	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[0]), '-C'), WT_B, 'the first writer is t2 (wt-b) — t1 was recognized as merged (receipt) and skipped');
  1879	  });
  1880	
  1881	  it('RED (I1a): a stale EMPTY branch at main tip (is-ancestor true, NO receipt) PARKS as stale — never auto-advances', async () => {
  1882	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'first' }, { id: 't2', title: 'second' }];
  1883	    // t1's branch is empty at main's tip: is-ancestor true, empty main..t1 — but
  1884	    // NO merge receipt (the writer crashed before its first commit).
  1885	    const git = pipelineGit({ mergedBranches: ['baton/wt-a/subtask-t1'] });
  1886	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), git, runner: undefined });
  1887	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
  1888	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1889	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 2 });
  1890	    // No receipt seeded.
  1891	
  1892	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1893	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a receipt-less is-ancestor branch parks as stale rather than silently skipping the subtask');
  1894	    const out = io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText();
  1895	    assert.match(out, /stale/i, 'the park names the stale branch');
  1896	    assert.equal(io.__git.matching(/^merge baton\//).length, 0, 'nothing is merged for a stale empty branch');
  1897	    // J2: the message must hedge BOTH causes — each pinned SEPARATELY so a
  1898	    // single-cause misdiagnosis cannot pass — point at the range to inspect, and
  1899	    // account for the branch still being checked out in the writer seat (a bare
  1900	    // "delete the branch" fails while it is checked out).
  1901	    assert.match(out, /crashed before committing|before.*first commit|pre-?commit/i, 'names the pre-commit-crash cause');
  1902	    assert.match(out, /merged without a receipt|merge.*receipt.*missing|not.*recorded/i, 'ALSO names the merged-without-receipt cause (no single-cause misdiagnosis)');
  1903	    assert.match(out, /git log main\.\.baton\/wt-a\/subtask-t1/, 'points at `git log main..<branch>` to disambiguate');
  1904	    assert.match(out, /seat|worktree|checked out|checkout/i, 'remediation accounts for the branch still being checked out in the writer seat');
  1905	  });
  1906	
  1907	  it('GUARD (I1 conjunction): a receipt WITHOUT is-ancestor does NOT auto-advance — the subtask is re-run, never silently skipped', async () => {
  1908	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'first' }, { id: 't2', title: 'second' }];
  1909	    // A receipt exists for t1, but the branch is NOT an ancestor of main (the
  1910	    // merge did not actually land — e.g. main was rewound). A receipt-ONLY
  1911	    // advance would skip t1; the required conjunction re-runs it.
  1912	    const git = pipelineGit({ mergedBranches: [] }); // is-ancestor FALSE for every branch
  1913	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), git, runner: undefined });
  1914	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
  1915	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1916	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 2 });
  1917	    seedReceipt(io, 't1', 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1'); // receipt present, ancestor absent
  1918	
  1919	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1920	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the run completes by re-running t1; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1921	    const writers = writersOf(io.__runner);
  1922	    assert.ok(writers.length >= 1, 'a writer ran');
  1923	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[0]), '-C'), WT_A, 'the FIRST writer is t1 (wt-a) — a receipt alone did NOT skip it (is-ancestor is a required conjunct)');
  1924	  });
  1925	
  1926	  it('RED (I1c): a clean merge PERSISTS a durable receipt {subtaskId, branch, mergedAt} BEFORE the phase-advance is journaled', async () => {
  1927	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
  1928	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1929	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1930	
  1931	    // Ordering hook: at each phase-advance journal write, capture whether the t1
  1932	    // receipt is ALREADY on disk (durable before the advance, not merely by EOR).
  1933	    const journalPath = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/journal.ndjson`;
  1934	    const receiptAtAdvance = [];
  1935	    const realAppend = io.fs.appendFileSync.bind(io.fs);
  1936	    io.fs.appendFileSync = (/** @type {any} */ path, /** @type {any} */ data) => {
  1937	      if (String(path) === journalPath && /"type":"phase-advance"/.test(String(data))) {
  1938	        receiptAtAdvance.push(receipts(io).some((r) => r.subtaskId === 't1'));
  1939	      }
  1940	      return realAppend(path, data);
  1941	    };
  1942	
  1943	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1944	    assert.equal(code, 0);
  1945	    const rec = receipts(io).find((r) => r.subtaskId === 't1');
  1946	    assert.ok(rec, 'a merge receipt is persisted after the subtask merges');
  1947	    assert.equal(rec.branch, 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1', 'the receipt names the merged branch');
  1948	    assert.ok(typeof rec.mergedAt === 'string' && rec.mergedAt.length > 0, 'the receipt records mergedAt');
  1949	    assert.ok(receiptAtAdvance.length >= 1 && receiptAtAdvance.every(Boolean), 'the receipt is durable on disk BEFORE the phase advance is journaled (crash-safe)');
  1950	  });
  1951	});
  1952	
  1953	// ===========================================================================
  1954	// I3 (A2/B4) — an EXISTING unstamped state (no flavor/specDigest) is refused by
  1955	// pipeline run, with the archive instruction, zero spawns.
  1956	describe('pipeline — I3: unstamped legacy state is refused', () => {
  1957	  it('RED (I3): an unstamped state.json (no flavor/specDigest) refuses exit 2 with the archive instruction, zero spawns', async () => {
  1958	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'a' }, { id: 't2', title: 'b' }] }), runner: undefined });
  1959	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
  1960	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1961	    io.fs.mkdirSync(loopPaths('/repo').dir, { recursive: true });
  1962	    io.fs.writeFileSync(
  1963	      loopPaths('/repo').state,
  1964	      JSON.stringify({
  1965	        schema: 'baton/loop-state@1', runId: 'legacy', goal: 'Ship the pipeline', phaseCount: 2, phaseIndex: 1,
  1966	        iterations: {}, status: 'running', parkReason: null, escalation: null, smokeApproval: null, createdAt: T0, journalSeq: 0,
  1967	        // NO flavor, NO specDigest.
  1968	      }, null, 2) + '\n',
  1969	    );
  1970	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1971	    assert.equal(code, 2, 'an unstamped state is refused, not resumed');
  1972	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /archive/i, 'the refusal tells the operator to archive the state');
  1973	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned on an unstamped-state refusal');
  1974	  });
  1975	});
  1976	
  1977	// ===========================================================================
  1978	// J1 (B1) — `baton pipeline resume` mirrors G6's loop resume: PARKED -> running
  1979	// (cap counters intact), ESCALATED refused, flavor-guarded.
  1980	describe('pipeline — J1: pipeline resume', () => {
  1981	  const gate = 'subtask-t1-review';
  1982	
  1983	  it('RED (J1a): resume transitions a PARKED run to running — the same subtask re-runs its writer, gate counters intact (no refund)', async () => {
  1984	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];
  1985	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), runner: undefined });
  1986	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1987	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1988	    // A parked run mid-subtask t1 with the gate already iterated 3 times.
  1989	    seedPipelineState(io, {
  1990	      flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 1,
  1991	      status: 'parked', parkReason: 'operator paused', iterations: { [gate]: 3 },
  1992	    });
  1993	
  1994	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'resume'], io);
  1995	    assert.notEqual(code, 2, 'resume is a recognized subcommand (not a usage error)');
  1996	    const writers = writersOf(io.__runner);
  1997	    assert.ok(writers.length >= 1, 'resume re-runs the parked subtask — its writer spawns again');
  1998	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[0]), '-C'), WT_A, 'the re-run writer is t1 in wt-a (not skipped)');
  1999	    assert.ok((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.iterations[gate] >= 3, 'the gate cap counter survives resume — no refund');
  2000	    // J1a: resume must pin SUCCESSFUL continuation of the clean case, not merely
  2001	    // "not-unknown + writer respawns". The clean subtask completes: exit 0, the
  2002	    // run reaches DONE, and the journal records a RESUME event BEFORE completion.
  2003	    assert.equal(code, 0, `resume completes the clean subtask; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  2004	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the resumed pipeline reaches DONE');
  2005	    const journal = (io.files()[loopPaths('/repo').journal] ?? '')
  2006	      .split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
  2007	    const resumeIdx = journal.findIndex((e) => e.type === LOOP_EVENT.RESUME);
  2008	    assert.ok(resumeIdx >= 0, 'the resume is journaled (a RESUME event is appended)');
  2009	    const advanceIdx = journal.findIndex((e) => e.type === LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE);
  2010	    assert.ok(advanceIdx > resumeIdx, 'the RESUME event precedes the phase-advance that completes the run');
  2011	  });
  2012	
  2013	  it('RED (J1b): resume refuses an ESCALATED run (exit 3, operator-only), zero spawns', async () => {
  2014	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];
  2015	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), runner: undefined });
  2016	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  2017	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2018	    seedPipelineState(io, {
  2019	      flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 1,
  2020	      status: 'escalated', escalation: { gate, iteration: 5 },
  2021	    });
  2022	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'resume'], io);
  2023	    assert.equal(code, 3, 'an escalated run cannot be resumed (operator-only)');
  2024	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'resume never spawns on an escalated run');
  2025	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, 'the run stays escalated');
  2026	  });
  2027	
  2028	  it('RED (J1c): pipeline resume refuses a loop-flavored state (exit 2, flavor guard), zero spawns', async () => {
  2029	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];
  2030	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), runner: undefined });
  2031	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  2032	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2033	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'loop', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 1, status: 'parked', parkReason: 'x' });
  2034	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'resume'], io);
  2035	    assert.equal(code, 2, 'a loop-flavored state cannot be resumed by pipeline');
  2036	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /flavor|loop|mismatch/i, 'the refusal names the flavor mismatch');
  2037	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned on a flavor mismatch');
  2038	  });
  2039	
  2040	  it('GREEN (J1d companion): `baton loop resume` still refuses a pipeline-flavored state (exit 2), zero spawns — the boundary holds both ways', async () => {
  2041	    // A loop repo (loop.json with phases) whose state was stamped by a pipeline run.
  2042	    const loopJson = { schema: 'baton/loop@1', goal: 'boundary', constraints: [], budgets: { iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 }, phases: [{ id: 'plan', role: 'planner' }, { id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }] };
  2043	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: loopJson, runner: undefined });
  2044	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  2045	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2046	    io.fs.mkdirSync(loopPaths('/repo').dir, { recursive: true });
  2047	    io.fs.writeFileSync(
  2048	      loopPaths('/repo').state,
  2049	      JSON.stringify({
  2050	        schema: 'baton/loop-state@1', runId: 'x', goal: 'boundary', phaseCount: 2, phaseIndex: 0,
  2051	        iterations: {}, status: 'parked', parkReason: 'x', escalation: null, smokeApproval: null, createdAt: T0, journalSeq: 0,
  2052	        flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(loopJson.phases),
  2053	      }, null, 2) + '\n',
  2054	    );
  2055	    const code = await run(['loop', 'resume'], io);
  2056	    assert.equal(code, 2, 'loop resume refuses a pipeline-flavored state');
  2057	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /flavor|pipeline|mismatch/i, 'the refusal names the flavor mismatch');
  2058	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned across the flavor boundary');
  2059	  });
  2060	
  2061	  it('RED (J1e): resume with NOTHING to resume REFUSES (exit 2) and never initializes a fresh run', async () => {
  2062	    // A repo with a spec (loop.json) but NO persisted .handoff/loop/state.json —
  2063	    // e.g. `resume` run in the wrong directory. Resuming must refuse, NOT quietly
  2064	    // init a new state and launch a full pipeline from scratch.
  2065	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
  2066	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  2067	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2068	    assert.equal(io.fs.existsSync(loopPaths('/repo').state), false, 'precondition: no persisted run state exists');
  2069	
  2070	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'resume'], io);
  2071	    assert.equal(code, 2, 'nothing to resume is a usage error, not a silent fresh run');
  2072	    const out = io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText();
  2073	    assert.match(out, /nothing to resume/i, 'the refusal states there is nothing to resume');
  2074	    assert.match(out, /baton pipeline run/, 'the refusal points at `baton pipeline run` to start a fresh run');
  2075	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'a nothing-to-resume refusal spawns no children');
  2076	    // The teeth: a wrong-directory resume must not initialize and start over.
  2077	    assert.equal(io.fs.existsSync(loopPaths('/repo').state), false, 'refusing to resume never creates a state.json');
  2078	  });
  2079	});

  2082	  return io.files()[`${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/${name}`];

    14	// modules (spec/state/children/failover) over the real command machinery.
    15	// Source: docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md §"baton loop
    16	// (Layer 2)", §"Smoke gate", §"Caps", §"Supervisor lifetime and recovery".

    19	//   io.superviseChild ?? superviseChild
    20	// so tests inject a scripted fake runner that returns the next queued result
    21	// and records the {command, args, env} it was invoked with. Everything else
    22	// drives the real modules over memfs.

    24	// The `run` subcommand does not exist yet: `loop run` currently exits 2
    25	// ("unknown subcommand 'run'"). Reds assert TARGET behavior (exit codes, spawn
    26	// counts, persisted state, ESCALATION/SMOKE-REVIEW files) — distinguished from
    27	// today's generic exit 2. --detach is OUT of unit scope (deferred to e2e).
    28	//
    29	// PINNED EXIT CODES: done 0 · awaiting-smoke-approval 0 · escalated 3 ·
    30	// parked 4 · live-lock refusal 1 · usage error 2.

    94	/**
    95	 * Scripted fake child runner honoring the injection seam. Each call shifts the
    96	 * next result off the queue, writes its logContent to opts.logPath (so the
    97	 * command reads the transcript from the child's LOG file), and records the
    98	 * spawn spec.
    99	 */
   100	function fakeRunner(io, script) {
   101	  const queue = [...script];
   102	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   103	  const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
   104	    calls.push({ spec, opts, args: spec?.args ?? [], command: spec?.command, env: spec?.env });
   105	    const r = queue.shift() ?? { timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'APPROVED', findings: '' };
   106	    // No fallback: the command MUST pass a real logPath (asserted below).
   107	    const logPath = opts.logPath;
   108	    if (typeof r.logContent === 'string') {
   109	      const dir = logPath.slice(0, logPath.lastIndexOf('/'));
   110	      io.fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   111	      io.fs.writeFileSync(logPath, r.logContent);
   112	    }
   113	    return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: r.timedOut ?? false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath });
   114	  };
   115	  fn.calls = calls;
   116	  return fn;
   117	}
   118	
   119	function makeLoopRepo({ spec = loopSpec(), files = {}, runner, execFile, env = {} } = {}) {
   120	  const io = makeIo({
   121	    now: T0,
   122	    host: 'loop-host',
   123	    pid: 4242,
   124	    startTime: 111000,
   125	    fencingSeed: 1,
   126	    env,
   127	    files: {
   128	      [SIG_ABS]: SIG_CONTENT,
   129	      '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(CONFIG, null, 2),
   130	      ...(spec ? { '/repo/loop.json': JSON.stringify(spec, null, 2) + '\n' } : {}),
   131	      ...files,
   132	    },
   133	  });
   134	  if (execFile) io.execFile = execFile;
   135	  const fake = runner ?? fakeRunner(io, []);
   136	  io.superviseChild = fake;
   137	  io.__runner = fake;
   138	  return io;
   139	}
   140	
   141	const argsOf = (io, i) => (io.__runner.calls[i]?.args ?? []).map(String);
   142	const serialize = (io, i) => argsOf(io, i).join(' ');
   143	const valAfter = (arr, flag) => { const i = arr.indexOf(flag); return i >= 0 ? arr[i + 1] : undefined; };
   144	const loopFile = (io, name) => io.files()[`${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/${name}`];
   145

   181	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned while another supervisor is live');
   182	  });
   183

   187	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;

   194	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'a dead lock is reclaimed and the run completes');

   199	// ===========================================================================
   200	describe('loop run — phase driving', () => {
   201	  it('a 2-phase run (plan -> gate-1), both APPROVED: children on the RESOLVED assignments, status done, state persisted', async () => {
   202	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   203	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   204	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   205	
   206	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   207	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'a fully-APPROVED run finishes clean');
   208	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 2, 'exactly one child per phase');
   209	
   210	    // Phase 1: planner -> claude-code (chain head), headless -p.
   211	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls[0].command, 'claude');
   212	    assert.ok(argsOf(io, 0).includes('-p'), 'the planner child runs headless');
   213	    // Phase 2: plan-reviewer -> codex/gpt-5.6-sol, reviewer read-only sandbox.
   214	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls[1].command, 'codex');
   215	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(io, 1), '--model'), 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'the reviewer child carries the resolved model');
   216	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(io, 1), '-s'), 'read-only', 'the reviewer runs in a read-only sandbox');
   217	
   218	    // Every spawn is given a real per-child log path under .handoff/loop/children/
   219	    // plus its platform and timeout/grace budget.
   220	    for (const [i] of io.__runner.calls.entries()) {
   221	      const opts = io.__runner.calls[i].opts;
   222	      assert.match(String(opts.logPath), /\/\.handoff\/loop\/children\//, `child ${i} log path is under .handoff/loop/children/`);
   223	      assert.ok(typeof opts.platform === 'string' && opts.platform.length > 0, `child ${i} opts carry the platform`);
   224	      assert.ok(typeof opts.timeoutMs === 'number' && opts.timeoutMs > 0, `child ${i} opts carry a timeout`);
   225	      assert.ok(typeof opts.graceMs === 'number' && opts.graceMs > 0, `child ${i} opts carry a grace period`);
   226	    }
   227	
   228	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   229	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the run reached done');
   230	    assert.equal(state.phaseIndex, 2, 'both phases advanced');
   231	    assert.ok(loopFile(io, 'state.json'), 'loop state persisted under .handoff/loop');
   232	    assert.ok(io.fs.existsSync(loopPaths('/repo').journal), 'the loop journal persisted');
   233	  });
   234	});
   235	
   236	// ===========================================================================
   237	describe('loop run — gate iteration + the 5-cap', () => {
   238	  const gateSpec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }] });
   239	
   240	  it('BLOCKED re-runs with findings in the retry prompt; the 6th attempt NEVER spawns; escalated + ESCALATION.md; exit 3', async () => {
   241	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: gateSpec, runner: undefined });
   242	    // Six BLOCKED results queued; only five may ever be consumed.
   243	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   244	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'FINDING-ALPHA-1' },
   245	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'FINDING-BETA-2' },
   246	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f3' },
   247	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f4' },
   248	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f5' },
   249	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f6' },
   250	    ]);
   251	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   252	
   253	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   254	    assert.equal(code, 3, 'escalation exits 3 (defined nonzero)');
   255	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 5, 'exactly five gate attempts — a 6th is never spawned');
   256	    // Each retry carries the PRECEDING attempt's findings — later findings are
   257	    // not dropped in favor of the first.
   258	    assert.match(serialize(io, 1), /FINDING-ALPHA-1/, "the 2nd attempt's prompt carries the 1st attempt's findings");
   259	    assert.match(serialize(io, 2), /FINDING-BETA-2/, "the 3rd attempt's prompt carries the 2nd attempt's findings (continuity, not just the first)");
   260	
   261	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   262	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, 'the run escalated at the cap');
   263	    const esc = loopFile(io, 'ESCALATION.md');
   264	    assert.ok(esc, 'an ESCALATION.md was written under .handoff/loop');
   265	    assert.match(esc, /gate-1/, 'the escalation names the exhausted gate');
   266	  });
   267	});
   268	
   269	// ===========================================================================
   270	describe('loop run — smoke gate', () => {
   271	  const smokeSpec = loopSpec({
   272	    smoke: { cmd: 'npm run smoke', expect: 'ok' },
   273	    phases: [
   274	      { id: 'smoke-tests', role: 'test-author' },
   275	      { id: 'smoke-verify', role: 'test-verifier' },
   276	      { id: 'smoke-build', role: 'implementer' },
   277	      { id: 'subtask-implement', role: 'implementer' },
   278	    ],
   279	  });
   280	
   281	  function smokeExecFile(rec) {
   282	    return (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = []) => {
   283	      rec.push([cmd, ...args].join(' '));
   284	      // git stays unavailable; the smoke command "passes".
   285	      if (cmd === 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
   286	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'ok\n', stderr: '' });
   287	    };
   288	  }
   289	
   290	  it('after smoke-build: runs smoke.cmd, writes SMOKE-REVIEW.md + a smoke-approval token, awaits, exit 0, no further child', async () => {
   291	    const rec = [];
   292	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: smokeSpec, execFile: smokeExecFile(rec), runner: undefined });
   293	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   294	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   295	
   296	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   297	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'pausing for smoke approval is a clean exit 0');
   298	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 3, 'the smoke slice ran (3 phases); the post-smoke phase did NOT spawn');
   299	    assert.ok(rec.some((c) => /smoke/.test(c)), 'smoke.cmd was executed via io.execFile');
   300	
   301	    assert.ok(loopFile(io, 'SMOKE-REVIEW.md'), 'SMOKE-REVIEW.md was written');
   302	    const approval = loopFile(io, 'smoke-approval.json');
   303	    assert.ok(approval, 'a smoke-approval record was written');
   304	    const token = JSON.parse(approval).token;
   305	    assert.ok(typeof token === 'string' && token.startsWith('smk1.'), 'the approval carries a smokeApprovalToken over the six inputs');
   306	
   307	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   308	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL, 'the run is awaiting smoke approval');
   309	  });
   310	
   311	  it('--approve-smoke <correct token> resumes into the next phase (a child spawns again)', async () => {
   312	    const rec = [];
   313	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: smokeSpec, execFile: smokeExecFile(rec), runner: undefined });
   314	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   315	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   316	    assert.equal(await cmdLoop(['run'], io), 0);
   317	    const token = JSON.parse(loopFile(io, 'smoke-approval.json')).token;
   318	
   319	    // Resume with the correct token — a FRESH runner for the continued phase.
   320	    const runner2 = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   321	    io.superviseChild = runner2;
   322	    io.__runner = runner2;
   323	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--approve-smoke', token], io);
   324	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   325	    assert.equal(runner2.calls.length, 1, 'a verified approval continues into the post-smoke phase');
   326	  });
   327	
   328	  it('--approve-smoke with the REAL issued token after a bound input drifted -> refused: exit 4, PARKED, zero spawns', async () => {
   329	    // A git-aware execFile whose HEAD is a mutable ref — HEAD is a bound token
   330	    // input, so moving it between issuance and approval is genuine drift.
   331	    let head = 'HEAD-A';
   332	    const gitAware = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = []) => {
   333	      if (cmd === 'git') {
   334	        const key = args.join(' ');
   335	        const out = key === 'rev-parse HEAD' ? head : key === 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD' ? 'main' : '';
   336	        return Promise.resolve({ stdout: out + '\n', stderr: '' });
   337	      }
   338	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'ok\n', stderr: '' }); // the smoke command
   339	    };
   340	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: smokeSpec, execFile: gitAware, runner: undefined });
   341	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   342	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   343	    assert.equal(await cmdLoop(['run'], io), 0);
   344	    const token = JSON.parse(loopFile(io, 'smoke-approval.json')).token; // the ACTUAL issued smk1 token
   345	
   346	    // Drift a bound input: HEAD moves after the human looked.
   347	    head = 'HEAD-B';
   348	    const runner2 = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   349	    io.superviseChild = runner2;
   350	    io.__runner = runner2;
   351	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--approve-smoke', token], io); // the OLD token, now stale
   352	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'the plan parks on smoke-token drift');
   353	    assert.equal(runner2.calls.length, 0, 'a drifted approval spawns no child');
   354	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   355	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'drift parks the run (not left silently awaiting)');
   356	  });
   357	});
   358	
   359	// ===========================================================================
   360	// D9 (v1.1 item 1) — done-status persistence. A smoke-approve resume that
   361	// completes the FINAL phase must persist raw status 'done'. Today the last
   362	// phase's PHASE_ADVANCE flips DONE, SMOKE_AWAIT overwrites it, and SMOKE_APPROVE
   363	// resumes to RUNNING — nothing re-flips DONE, so the completion path prints
   364	// done + exits 0 while state.json stays 'running'.
   365	describe('loop run — D9: done-status persistence', () => {
   366	  const smokeExec = (rec = []) => (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = []) => {
   367	    rec.push([cmd, ...args].join(' '));
   368	    if (cmd === 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
   369	    return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'ok\n', stderr: '' });
   370	  };
   371	  // smoke-build is the LAST phase — no phase after the smoke gate re-flips DONE.
   372	  const finalSmokeSpec = loopSpec({
   373	    smoke: { cmd: 'npm run smoke', expect: 'ok' },
   374	    phases: [{ id: 'work', role: 'implementer' }, { id: 'smoke-build', role: 'implementer' }],
   375	  });
   376	
   377	  it('RED (D9): a smoke-approved run that completes its FINAL phase persists raw status done', async () => {
   378	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: finalSmokeSpec, execFile: smokeExec(), runner: undefined });
   379	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]); // work + smoke-build
   380	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   381	    assert.equal(await cmdLoop(['run'], io), 0, 'the first pass reaches the smoke gate');
   382	    const token = JSON.parse(loopFile(io, 'smoke-approval.json')).token;
   383	
   384	    const runner2 = fakeRunner(io, []); // no phase remains after the smoke gate
   385	    io.superviseChild = runner2;
   386	    io.__runner = runner2;
   387	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--approve-smoke', token], io);
   388	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'the approved completion exits 0');
   389	    const raw = JSON.parse(loopFile(io, 'state.json'));
   390	    assert.equal(raw.status, 'done', 'the completed run persists status done (not left running after SMOKE_APPROVE)');
   391	  });
   392	
   393	  it('GUARD (D9): re-running a completed (done) run still prints done and exits 0, no re-spawn', async () => {
   394	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'work', role: 'implementer' }] });
   395	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
   396	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, []);
   397	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   398	    const p = loopPaths('/repo');
   399	    io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
   400	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${p.dir}/state.json`, JSON.stringify({
   401	      schema: 'baton/loop-state@1', runId: 'loop-done', goal: 'Ship the loop', phaseCount: 1, phaseIndex: 1,
   402	      iterations: {}, status: 'done', parkReason: null, escalation: null, smokeApproval: null, createdAt: T0, journalSeq: 0,
   403	      flavor: 'loop', specDigest: dedupeKey(spec.phases),
   404	    }, null, 2) + '\n');
   405	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   406	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'a re-run of a done run exits 0');
   407	    assert.match(io.stdoutText(), /done/, 'it still prints done');
   408	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child re-spawns for a completed run');
   409	  });
   410	});
   411	
   412	// ===========================================================================
   413	// ITEM 3 (v1.1) — per-phase child budgets. Today budgets.maxChildrenPerPhase
   414	// gates a GLOBAL product (childSeq >= maxChildrenPerPhase * phases.length);
   415	// enforce it PER phase so one runaway phase parks naming itself while others are
   416	// unaffected, and the SUM across phases may legitimately exceed a single budget.
   417	describe('loop run — per-phase child budgets (item 3)', () => {
   418	  // A runner that ALWAYS returns BLOCKED — the phase never passes on its own.
   419	  const alwaysBlocked = (io) => {
   420	    const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   421	    const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts) => {
   422	      calls.push({ spec, command: spec?.command, args: spec?.args ?? [] });
   423	      return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'still-not-done', logPath: opts.logPath });
   424	    };
   425	    fn.calls = calls;
   426	    return fn;
   427	  };
   428	
   429	  it('RED (item 3): a phase exhausting its OWN child budget parks naming that phase (global product NOT exhausted)', async () => {
   430	    // 3 phases → the GLOBAL product is 2*3 = 6, far from exhausted; gate-1 alone
   431	    // must park at its OWN budget of 2, before the 5-iteration cap trips.
   432	    const spec = loopSpec({
   433	      budgets: { iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 2 },
   434	      phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }, { id: 'plan', role: 'planner' }, { id: 'gate-2', role: 'plan-reviewer' }],
   435	    });
   436	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
   437	    const runner = alwaysBlocked(io);
   438	    io.superviseChild = runner;
   439	    io.__runner = runner;
   440	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   441	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'exhausting a single phase’s child budget parks (exit 4), not escalates at the cap');
   442	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   443	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'the run is parked');
   444	    assert.match(state.parkReason ?? '', /gate-1/, 'the park reason names the phase whose budget was exhausted');
   445	    // The phase SPENT its full budget (not an immediate/early park): exactly 2
   446	    // children ran, each a BLOCKED gate iteration, so the budget — not something
   447	    // else — is the binding constraint.
   448	    assert.equal(runner.calls.length, 2, `the phase spent exactly its 2-child budget before parking; got ${runner.calls.length}`);
   449	    assert.equal(state.iterations['gate-1'], 2, 'both budgeted children ran their BLOCKED gate iterations (iterations[gate-1] === 2)');
   450	  });
   451	
   452	  it('GUARD (item 3): phases each UNDER their own budget complete even when the SUM exceeds one phase budget', async () => {
   453	    // 3 phases, budget 2 each. Each spends 2 children (BLOCKED then APPROVED) —
   454	    // total 6 across phases > any single budget (2), and > a naive global 2.
   455	    const spec = loopSpec({
   456	      budgets: { iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 2 },
   457	      phases: [{ id: 'p1', role: 'planner' }, { id: 'p2', role: 'planner' }, { id: 'p3', role: 'planner' }],
   458	    });
   459	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
   460	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   461	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'x' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // p1: 2 children
   462	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'x' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // p2: 2 children
   463	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'x' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // p3: 2 children
   464	    ]);
   465	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   466	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   467	    assert.equal(code, 0, `no per-phase budget is exceeded, so the run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   468	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the run reached DONE');
   469	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 6, 'the SUM of children (6) exceeds any single phase budget (2) — no global-product regression');
   470	  });
   471	});
   472	
   473	// ===========================================================================
   474	// ITEM 4 (v1.1) — findings persistence. Append-only .handoff/loop/findings/
   475	// <gate>.ndjson, one line per BLOCKED verdict {iteration?, findings, at}; the
   476	// next child prompt for that gate embeds the LATEST persisted findings, and
   477	// escalations embed them too — so a park→resume or a crash→re-invoke re-prompts
   478	// with the real prior findings instead of an empty string.
   479	describe('loop run — findings persistence (item 4)', () => {
   480	  const P = loopPaths('/repo');
   481	  const findingsLines = (io, gate) => {
   482	    const raw = io.files()[`${P.dir}/findings/${gate}.ndjson`];
   483	    return typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l)) : [];
   484	  };
   485	  const seedRunningState = (io, over) => {
   486	    io.fs.mkdirSync(P.dir, { recursive: true });
   487	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${P.dir}/state.json`, JSON.stringify({
   488	      schema: 'baton/loop-state@1', runId: 'loop-item4', goal: 'Ship the loop', phaseCount: 1, phaseIndex: 0,
   489	      iterations: {}, status: 'running', parkReason: null, escalation: null, smokeApproval: null, createdAt: T0, journalSeq: 0,
   490	      flavor: 'loop', ...over,
   491	    }, null, 2) + '\n');
   492	  };
   493	
   494	  it('RED (4a): a BLOCKED gate then PARK then resume carries the persisted findings into the next child prompt', async () => {
   495	    const marker = 'DISTINCTIVE_FINDINGS_4A_MARKER';
   496	    const cfg = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(CONFIG));
   497	    cfg.roles['solo-codex'] = ['codex/gpt-5.6-sol@xhigh']; // single entry — a model death parks
   498	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'solo-codex' }] });
   499	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, files: { '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(cfg, null, 2), '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': JSON.stringify(ownedBundle('codex', 'open'), null, 2) + '\n' }, runner: undefined });
   500	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   501	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 0, findings: marker }, // records the gate findings
   502	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: `child died: ${MODEL_UNAVAIL}\n` }, // model death → single-entry chain parks
   503	    ]);
   504	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   505	    assert.equal(await cmdLoop(['run'], io), 4, 'run 1 parks after recording the BLOCKED findings');
   506	    assert.ok(findingsLines(io, 'gate-1').some((l) => String(l.findings).includes(marker)), 'the BLOCKED findings were persisted to findings/gate-1.ndjson');
   507	
   508	    const runner2 = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   509	    io.superviseChild = runner2;
   510	    io.__runner = runner2;
   511	    await cmdLoop(['resume'], io);
   512	    assert.ok(runner2.calls.length >= 1, 'resume re-spawned the gate child');
   513	    assert.match((runner2.calls[0].args ?? []).map(String).join(' '), new RegExp(marker), 'the resumed child prompt embeds the persisted prior findings');
   514	  });
   515	
   516	  // Two ordered persisted lines — the LATEST must win, the older must not be
   517	  // selected as the active prior findings.
   518	  const OLD = 'OLDER_FINDINGS_SUPERSEDED';
   519	  const NEW = 'NEWER_FINDINGS_LATEST';
   520	  const seedTwoFindings = (io, gate) => {
   521	    io.fs.mkdirSync(`${P.dir}/findings`, { recursive: true });
   522	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${P.dir}/findings/${gate}.ndjson`,
   523	      JSON.stringify({ iteration: 1, findings: OLD, at: T0 }) + '\n' + JSON.stringify({ iteration: 2, findings: NEW, at: T0 }) + '\n');
   524	  };
   525	
   526	  it('RED (4b loop): a re-invoke embeds the LATEST persisted findings in the FIRST child prompt (older superseded)', async () => {
   527	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }] });
   528	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
   529	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   530	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   531	    seedRunningState(io, { specDigest: dedupeKey(spec.phases), iterations: { 'gate-1': 2 } });
   532	    seedTwoFindings(io, 'gate-1');
   533	
   534	    await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   535	    assert.ok(io.__runner.calls.length >= 1, 'a child spawned on re-invoke');
   536	    const prompt = (io.__runner.calls[0].args ?? []).map(String).join(' ');
   537	    assert.match(prompt, new RegExp(NEW), 'the first re-invoke child prompt embeds the LATEST persisted findings');
   538	    assert.doesNotMatch(prompt, new RegExp(OLD), 'the superseded (older) findings is not selected as the active prior findings');
   539	  });
   540	
   541	  it('RED (4c loop): a fresh-invoke cap escalation embeds the LATEST persisted findings in ESCALATION.md (older superseded)', async () => {
   542	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }], budgets: { iterationCap: 1, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 } });
   543	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
   544	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]); // unused — escalates before spawn
   545	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   546	    seedRunningState(io, { specDigest: dedupeKey(spec.phases), iterations: { 'gate-1': 1 } }); // AT cap 1
   547	    seedTwoFindings(io, 'gate-1');
   548	
   549	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   550	    assert.equal(code, 3, 'a gate at the cap escalates');
   551	    const esc = loopFile(io, 'ESCALATION.md') ?? '';
   552	    assert.match(esc, new RegExp(NEW), 'ESCALATION.md embeds the LATEST persisted findings even when in-memory findings are empty');
   553	    assert.doesNotMatch(esc, new RegExp(OLD), 'the superseded (older) findings is not the one embedded');
   554	  });
   555	
   556	  it('RED (4d): the findings file is append-only across iterations (two BLOCKED verdicts → two ordered lines)', async () => {
   557	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }], budgets: { iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 } });
   558	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
   559	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   560	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'first-finding-A' },
   561	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'second-finding-B' },
   562	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' },
   563	    ]);
   564	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   565	    await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   566	    const lines = findingsLines(io, 'gate-1');
   567	    assert.equal(lines.length, 2, 'two BLOCKED verdicts append two findings lines (append-only, never overwrite)');
   568	    assert.match(String(lines[0].findings), /first-finding-A/, 'the first BLOCKED findings is line 1');
   569	    assert.match(String(lines[1].findings), /second-finding-B/, 'the second BLOCKED findings is line 2 (order preserved)');
   570	  });
   571	});
   572	
   573	// ===========================================================================
   574	// ITEM 4b (v1.1) — review artifacts from gate children. After EVERY verdict
   575	// parse for a gate child the supervisor writes reviews/<runId>/<gate>/
   576	// iteration-NN/{prompt.md, verdict.md} in the repo tree (layout per
   577	// reviews/README.md): prompt.md is the child prompt verbatim; verdict.md is a
   578	// header (role/model/date) + the parsed verdict + findings. NN = the gate
   579	// iteration count at parse time (01-based). Writes are FAIL-OPEN.
   580	describe('loop run — review artifacts from gate children (item 4b)', () => {
   581	  const promptOf = (call) => (call?.args ?? []).map(String).find((a) => a.includes('You are the')) ?? '';
   582	  // Parse a verdict.md into its leading `key: value` header block + trailing
   583	  // body (findings), per reviews/README.md's header convention.
   584	  const parseVerdictMd = (text) => {
   585	    const lines = String(text ?? '').split('\n');
   586	    const header = /** @type {Record<string,string>} */ ({});
   587	    let i = 0;
   588	    for (; i < lines.length; i += 1) {
   589	      const m = lines[i].match(/^([A-Za-z][\w-]*):\s?(.*)$/);
   590	      if (!m) break;
   591	      header[m[1].toLowerCase()] = m[2].trim();
   592	    }
   593	    return { header, body: lines.slice(i).join('\n').trim() };
   594	  };
   595	
   596	  it('RED (4b-1): a two-iteration gate leaves iteration-01/02 with verbatim prompt.md + full-contract verdict.md', async () => {
   597	    const marker = 'FIX_THE_THING_MARKER';
   598	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }] });
   599	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
   600	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   601	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: marker },
   602	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' },
   603	    ]);
   604	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   605	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   606	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the gate completes after the APPROVED; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   607	    const runId = (await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.runId;
   608	    const base = `/repo/reviews/${runId}/gate-1`;
   609	    const f = (rel) => io.files()[`${base}/${rel}`];
   610	
   611	    // iteration-01 — the BLOCKED attempt (01-based iteration count at parse time).
   612	    assert.ok(f('iteration-01/prompt.md'), 'iteration-01/prompt.md written');
   613	    assert.ok(f('iteration-01/verdict.md'), 'iteration-01/verdict.md written');
   614	    // prompt.md is EXACTLY the child prompt, verbatim.
   615	    assert.equal(f('iteration-01/prompt.md'), promptOf(io.__runner.calls[0]), 'iteration-01 prompt.md === the child prompt verbatim');
   616	    // verdict.md carries the FULL header contract + parsed verdict + findings.
   617	    const v1 = parseVerdictMd(f('iteration-01/verdict.md'));
   618	    assert.equal(v1.header.role, 'plan-reviewer', 'verdict header: exact role');
   619	    assert.match(v1.header.model ?? '', /gpt-5\.6-sol/, 'verdict header: concrete model');
   620	    assert.match(v1.header.model ?? '', /xhigh/, 'verdict header: the effort actually used');
   621	    assert.match(`${v1.header.harness ?? ''}${v1.header.platform ?? ''}`, /codex/i, 'verdict header: harness/platform');
   622	    assert.equal(v1.header.date, '2026-07-19', 'verdict header: date (YYYY-MM-DD from io.now())');
   623	    assert.equal(v1.header.verdict, 'BLOCKED', 'verdict header: exact verdict');
   624	    assert.ok('degraded' in v1.header, 'verdict header: degraded field present');
   625	    assert.equal(v1.body, marker, 'verdict.md body is the exact findings text');
   626	
   627	    // iteration-02 — the APPROVED attempt; its prompt embeds the prior findings.
   628	    assert.ok(f('iteration-02/prompt.md'), 'iteration-02/prompt.md written');
   629	    assert.equal(f('iteration-02/prompt.md'), promptOf(io.__runner.calls[1]), 'iteration-02 prompt.md === the second child prompt verbatim');
   630	    assert.match(f('iteration-02/prompt.md'), new RegExp(marker), 'the second prompt embeds the prior findings');
   631	    const v2 = parseVerdictMd(f('iteration-02/verdict.md'));
   632	    assert.equal(v2.header.role, 'plan-reviewer', 'iteration-02 verdict header: exact role');
   633	    assert.match(v2.header.model ?? '', /gpt-5\.6-sol/, 'iteration-02 verdict header: concrete model');
   634	    assert.match(v2.header.model ?? '', /xhigh/, 'iteration-02 verdict header: the effort actually used');
   635	    assert.match(`${v2.header.harness ?? ''}${v2.header.platform ?? ''}`, /codex/i, 'iteration-02 verdict header: harness/platform');
   636	    assert.equal(v2.header.date, '2026-07-19', 'iteration-02 verdict header: exact date');
   637	    assert.equal(v2.header.verdict, 'APPROVED', 'iteration-02 verdict header: exact verdict');
   638	    assert.ok('degraded' in v2.header, 'iteration-02 verdict header: degraded field present');
   639	    assert.equal(v2.body, '', 'iteration-02 verdict.md body is the exact findings (empty for a clean APPROVED)');
   640	  });
   641	
   642	  it('RED (4b-2, fail-open): an artifact write error WARNS but never fails the run', async () => {
   643	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }] });
   644	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
   645	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   646	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   647	    // Every write under reviews/ throws — the run must not be failed by it.
   648	    const realWrite = io.fs.writeFileSync.bind(io.fs);
   649	    io.fs.writeFileSync = (/** @type {any} */ pth, /** @type {any} */ data, /** @type {any} */ o) => {
   650	      if (String(pth).includes('/reviews/')) throw Object.assign(new Error('EACCES: reviews/ not writable'), { code: 'EACCES' });
   651	      return realWrite(pth, data, o);
   652	    };
   653	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   654	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'an artifact write error does NOT fail the run (fail-open)');
   655	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the run still completed');
   656	    const runId = (await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.runId;
   657	    assert.match(io.stderrText(), new RegExp(`reviews/${runId}/gate-1/iteration-01/(prompt|verdict)\\.md`), 'the warning names the CONCRETE failed artifact path');
   658	  });
   659	
   660	  it('GUARD (4b): a DEAD (failover) gate child writes NO verdict.md — only parsed verdicts leave artifacts', async () => {
   661	    const cfg = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(CONFIG));
   662	    cfg.roles['solo-codex'] = ['codex/gpt-5.6-sol@xhigh']; // single entry — a model death parks
   663	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'solo-codex' }] });
   664	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, files: { '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(cfg, null, 2), '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': JSON.stringify(ownedBundle('codex', 'open'), null, 2) + '\n' }, runner: undefined });
   665	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: `child died: ${MODEL_UNAVAIL}\n` }]);
   666	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   667	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   668	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'the model-unavailable death parks');
   669	    const verdictArtifacts = Object.keys(io.files()).filter((k) => /\/reviews\/.*\/verdict\.md$/.test(k));
   670	    assert.deepEqual(verdictArtifacts, [], 'a child that produced no parsed verdict leaves no verdict.md');
   671	  });
   672	});
   673	
   674	// ===========================================================================
   675	// ITEM 8 (v1.1) — probe integration. Each invocation loads the doctor's cached
   676	// availability probes ONCE (readProbeCache, 15-min freshness) and passes that
   677	// snapshot as probes: into EVERY resolveRoles/runFailover call. A fresh cache
   678	// marking a platform rate-limited diverts resolution off it; a stale/missing
   679	// cache is null → today's offline-degraded behavior, byte-identical.
   680	describe('loop run — probe integration (item 8)', () => {
   681	  const CACHE = '/repo/.handoff/log/probe-cache.json';
   682	  const seedCache = (io, records, at = T0) => {
   683	    io.fs.mkdirSync('/repo/.handoff/log', { recursive: true });
   684	    io.fs.writeFileSync(CACHE, JSON.stringify({ at, records }));
   685	  };
   686	  const soloImpl = () => loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'work', role: 'implementer' }] });
   687	
   688	  it('RED (8-1): a fresh cache rate-limiting the head platform diverts the FIRST spawn to the fallback', async () => {
   689	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: soloImpl(), runner: undefined });
   690	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   691	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   692	    // implementer chain head is claude-code; rate-limit it → codex fallback.
   693	    seedCache(io, [{ platform: 'claude-code', capability: 'installed', outcome: 'rate-limited' }]);
   694	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   695	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the run completes on the fallback; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   696	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 1, 'one child spawned');
   697	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls[0].command, 'codex', 'the first spawn diverted to codex — the rate-limited claude-code never spawned');
   698	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(io, 0), '--model'), 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'the resolved model is the fallback chain entry');
   699	  });
   700	
   701	  it('RED (8-1 failover): a loop failover relaunch honors the cache — dead head avoided, rate-limited middle skipped for the surviving tail', async () => {
   702	    const cfg = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(CONFIG));
   703	    cfg.roles['trio'] = ['codex/cx-head@xhigh', 'claude-code/cc-mid', 'cursor/cu-tail'];
   704	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'work', role: 'trio' }] });
   705	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, files: { '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(cfg, null, 2) }, runner: undefined });
   706	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   707	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: `working…\n${MODEL_UNAVAIL}\n` }, // cx-head dies model-unavailable
   708	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // the relaunched child
   709	    ]);
   710	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   711	    seedCache(io, [{ platform: 'claude-code', capability: 'installed', outcome: 'rate-limited' }]);
   712	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   713	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the relaunch completes on the surviving tail; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   714	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 2, 'the head died and exactly one relaunch spawned');
   715	    assert.notEqual(io.__runner.calls[1].command, 'claude', 'the failover skipped the rate-limited claude-code middle entry');
   716	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(io, 1), '--model'), 'cu-tail', 'the relaunch landed on the surviving cursor tail (cache reached loop runFailover)');
   717	  });
   718	
   719	  it('GUARD (8-4): a STALE cache and a MISSING cache both change nothing — the head spawns as today', async () => {
   720	    const staleAt = new Date(Date.parse(T0) - 20 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(); // 20 min old, past the window
   721	    const ioStale = makeLoopRepo({ spec: soloImpl(), runner: undefined });
   722	    ioStale.superviseChild = fakeRunner(ioStale, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   723	    ioStale.__runner = ioStale.superviseChild;
   724	    seedCache(ioStale, [{ platform: 'claude-code', capability: 'installed', outcome: 'rate-limited' }], staleAt);
   725	    await cmdLoop(['run'], ioStale);
   726	    assert.equal(ioStale.__runner.calls[0].command, 'claude', 'a stale cache is ignored — the head (claude-code) spawns as today');
   727	
   728	    const ioNone = makeLoopRepo({ spec: soloImpl(), runner: undefined });
   729	    ioNone.superviseChild = fakeRunner(ioNone, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   730	    ioNone.__runner = ioNone.superviseChild;
   731	    await cmdLoop(['run'], ioNone);
   732	    assert.equal(ioNone.__runner.calls[0].command, 'claude', 'no cache file — the head spawns as today');
   733	  });
   734	
   735	  it('GUARD (8-5): a fresh cache marking the head merely unverifiable (installed/ok) still resolves it — degraded, never blocked', async () => {
   736	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: soloImpl(), runner: undefined });
   737	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   738	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   739	    seedCache(io, [{ platform: 'claude-code', capability: 'installed', outcome: 'ok' }]);
   740	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   741	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'an unverifiable-but-not-rate-limited head is not blocked');
   742	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls[0].command, 'claude', 'installed/ok is selectable (degraded) — the head still spawns, never skipped');
   743	  });
   744	});
   745	
   746	// ===========================================================================
   747	// ITEM 10 (v1.1) — --detach. `baton loop run --detach` forks a background
   748	// supervisor that survives the invoking terminal; the parent returns promptly
   749	// once the detached child owns the run lock, printing its pid + a tail hint.
   750	//
   751	// SEAM JUDGMENT CALL (flagged for the verifier): a REAL fork is not honestly
   752	// unit-testable (an orphaned process, timing), so these pin the behavior through
   753	// an injected `io.spawnDetached(spec) -> { pid }` seam the implementation should
   754	// honor (default: a real detached child_process.spawn). Assumptions pinned:
   755	//   - the PARENT checks the run lock (refuse-live / reclaim-dead) BEFORE forking;
   756	//   - the DETACHED CHILD owns the lock (the fake writes it naming the child pid);
   757	//   - spawnDetached receives the capped supervisor.out output path.
   758	// Windows: --detach is POSIX-only (documented unsupported) — SKIP_WIN.
   759	describe('loop run — --detach (item 10)', () => {
   760	  const SKIP_WIN = process.platform === 'win32';
   761	  const DIR = loopPaths('/repo').dir;
   762	  const detachSeam = (io, pid, { writeLock = true } = {}) => {
   763	    const rec = { calls: [] };
   764	    io.spawnDetached = (/** @type {any} */ spec) => {
   765	      rec.calls.push(spec);
   766	      if (writeLock) {
   767	        io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   768	        io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: io.host, pid, startTime: 1, runId: `sup-${pid}` }));
   769	      }
   770	      return { pid };
   771	    };
   772	    return rec;
   773	  };
   774	
   775	  it('RED (10-1): --detach forks a detached supervisor; the parent exits 0 after the child owns the lock, printing pid + tail hint', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
   776	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   777	    const DETACHED_PID = 424242;
   778	    const seam = detachSeam(io, DETACHED_PID);
   779	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--detach'], io);
   780	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the parent returns promptly with exit 0; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   781	    assert.equal(seam.calls.length, 1, 'the supervisor was forked via the detach seam exactly once');
   782	    const lock = JSON.parse(io.files()[`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`]);
   783	    assert.equal(lock.pid, DETACHED_PID, 'the run lock names the detached supervisor pid');
   784	    assert.match(io.stdoutText(), new RegExp(String(DETACHED_PID)), 'stdout prints the detached pid');
   785	    assert.match(io.stdoutText(), /supervisor\.out/, 'stdout prints a tail hint for .handoff/loop/supervisor.out');
   786	  });
   787	
   788	  it('RED (10-2): --detach refuses (exit 1) when a LIVE supervisor already holds the lock — no fork', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
   789	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   790	    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ pid) => pid === 55555;
   791	    const seam = detachSeam(io, 1);
   792	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   793	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 55555, startTime: 222, runId: 'live' }));
   794	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--detach'], io);
   795	    assert.equal(code, 1, 'a live lock refuses the detached run (exit 1)');
   796	    assert.equal(seam.calls.length, 0, 'no detached supervisor is forked while the lock is held live');
   797	  });
   798	
   799	  it('RED (10-3): --detach reclaims a DEAD lock, killing recorded in-flight child groups BEFORE forking', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
   800	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   801	    const kills = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   802	    io.processKill = (/** @type {number} */ pid, /** @type {any} */ sig) => { kills.push({ pid, sig }); return true; };
   803	    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ pid) => pid === 9001; // orphan group alive; lock pid 999999 dead
   804	    const seam = detachSeam(io, 7);
   805	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   806	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 999999, startTime: 7, runId: 'crashed' }));
   807	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/children.ndjson`, JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', pid: 8001, pgid: 9001, startedAt: T0 }) + '\n');
   808	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--detach'], io);
   809	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the reclaiming detached run starts; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   810	    assert.ok(kills.some((k) => k.pid === -9001), `the orphan group 9001 was killed on reclaim; kills: ${JSON.stringify(kills)}`);
   811	    assert.equal(seam.calls.length, 1, 'after reclaiming, the detached supervisor is forked');
   812	  });
   813	
   814	  it('GUARD (10-4): a completing run releases the lock — the release contract a detached supervisor reuses', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
   815	    // The detached child runs the same supervisor body; isolating its release
   816	    // needs a real child, so this pins the reused contract on the attached path.
   817	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'work', role: 'implementer' }] }), runner: undefined });
   818	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   819	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   820	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   821	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   822	    assert.ok(!io.fs.existsSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`), 'a completing run releases the supervisor.lock');
   823	  });
   824	
   825	  it('RED (10-5): --detach directs the detached output to a byte-capped .handoff/loop/supervisor.out', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
   826	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   827	    const seam = detachSeam(io, 7);
   828	    await cmdLoop(['run', '--detach'], io);
   829	    assert.equal(seam.calls.length, 1, 'the detach seam was invoked');
   830	    const spec = seam.calls[0];
   831	    assert.match(String(spec.outPath ?? spec.out ?? ''), /\.handoff\/loop\/supervisor\.out$/, 'the detached output is directed to .handoff/loop/supervisor.out');
   832	    const cap = spec.maxBytes ?? spec.cap ?? spec.capBytes;
   833	    assert.ok(typeof cap === 'number' && cap > 0, `a positive byte cap is passed for supervisor.out; got ${JSON.stringify(cap)}`);
   834	  });
   835	});
   836	
   837	// ===========================================================================
   838	describe('loop run — failover integration', () => {
   839	  it('a usage-limit child (limit banner in its LOG) relaunches on the NEW platform (next child argv)', async () => {
   840	    // implementer chain: [claude-code, codex, cursor]; the claude-code child hits
   841	    // a session limit -> failover avoids claude-code -> relaunch on codex.
   842	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'subtask-implement', role: 'implementer' }] });
   843	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, files: { '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': JSON.stringify(ownedBundle('claude-code', 'open'), null, 2) + '\n' }, runner: undefined });
   844	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   845	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: `child transcript...\n${CC_LIMIT}\n` }, // the limit lives in the LOG
   846	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // the relaunched child succeeds
   847	    ]);
   848	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   849	
   850	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   851	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 2, 'the failover relaunched exactly one follow-up child');
   852	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls[0].command, 'claude', 'the first child ran on the claude-code chain head');
   853	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls[1].command, 'codex', 'the relaunch ran on the failover target platform (codex)');
   854	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(io, 1), '--model'), 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'the relaunch used the resolved next-entry model');
   855	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'the relaunched child completed the phase');
   856	  });
   857	
   858	  it('a failover PARK decision stops the run parked (single-entry CODEX role, model-unavailable)', async () => {
   859	    // The model-unavailable signature is CODEX-only, and the classifier filters
   860	    // by the child's platform — so the dead child must run on codex. A
   861	    // single-entry codex chain exhausts after its dead model -> runFailover parks.
   862	    const cfg = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(CONFIG));
   863	    cfg.roles['solo-codex'] = ['codex/gpt-5.6-sol@xhigh'];
   864	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'work', role: 'solo-codex' }] });
   865	    const io = makeLoopRepo({
   866	      spec,
   867	      files: {
   868	        '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(cfg, null, 2),
   869	        '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': JSON.stringify(ownedBundle('codex', 'open'), null, 2) + '\n',
   870	      },
   871	      runner: undefined,
   872	    });
   873	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: `child said: ${MODEL_UNAVAIL}\n` }]);
   874	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   875	
   876	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   877	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 1, 'no relaunch when failover parks');
   878	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls[0].command, 'codex', 'the dead child genuinely ran on codex (so the codex-only signature applies)');
   879	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a parked run exits 4 (defined nonzero)');
   880	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   881	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'the run is parked');
   882	  });
   883	});
   884	
   885	// ===========================================================================
   886	// D1 (dogfood milestone C) — classification reads the transcript TAIL, not the
   887	// whole child log: a signature the child itself WROTE mid-log (documenting
   888	// failover in a diff) must not route a healthy exit-0 child into failover. Seam:
   889	// classify a bounded tail (~4 KB); pin the behavior (signature placed far from
   890	// the end), not the constant.
   891	describe('loop run — D1: classification uses the transcript tail, not the body', () => {
   892	  const bodySignatureLog = (signature) =>
   893	    `writing docs…\n${signature}\n` +
   894	    '+ a clean documented line\n'.repeat(600) + // ~15 KB of body, well past any ~4 KB tail
   895	    `\ntokens used: 88\nDone.\nVERDICT: APPROVED\nFINDINGS: none\n`;
   896	
   897	  it('RED (D1): a child whose LOG BODY carries a usage-limit signature but whose exit-0 TAIL is clean advances the phase (NO failover)', async () => {
   898	    // A single non-gate phase: an OK child just advances to done.
   899	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'plan', role: 'planner' }] }); // planner = claude-code
   900	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
   901	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   902	      { verdict: 'APPROVED', exitCode: 0, logContent: bodySignatureLog(CC_LIMIT) },
   903	    ]);
   904	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   905	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   906	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the phase completes — the body signature was NOT a death; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   907	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 1, 'exactly one child — no failover relaunch on the child’s own work product');
   908	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the run reached DONE');
   909	  });
   910	
   911	  it('GUARD (D1): a death banner in the TAIL still classifies and routes to failover (parks a single-entry codex chain)', async () => {
   912	    const cfg = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(CONFIG));
   913	    cfg.roles['solo-codex'] = ['codex/gpt-5.6-sol@xhigh'];

   917	      files: { '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(cfg, null, 2), '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': JSON.stringify(ownedBundle('codex', 'open'), null, 2) + '\n' },
   918	      runner: undefined,

   920	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [

   922	    ]);
   923	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;

   926	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'the run parked (failover was consulted)');
   927	  });
   928	});

   951	// ===========================================================================
   952	describe('loop run — recovery from a mid-run crash', () => {
   953	  it('resumes from the journal position: only the REMAINING phase spawns a child', async () => {
   954	    const spec = loopSpec(); // plan -> gate-1
   955	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
   956	    // Seed a mid-run state: plan already completed (phaseIndex 1), gate-1 pending,
   957	    // plus a matching journal and a DEAD supervisor lock.
   958	    const p = loopPaths('/repo');
   959	    io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
   960	    const midState = {
   961	      schema: 'baton/loop-state@1',
   962	      runId: 'loop-prior',
   963	      goal: 'Ship the loop',
   964	      phaseCount: 2,
   965	      phaseIndex: 1,
   966	      iterations: { 'gate-1': 0 },
   967	      status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING,
   968	      parkReason: null,
   969	      escalation: null,
   970	      smokeApproval: null,
   971	      createdAt: T0,
   972	      journalSeq: 1,

   974	      flavor: 'loop',
   975	      specDigest: dedupeKey(spec.phases),
   976	    };
   977	    io.fs.writeFileSync(p.state, JSON.stringify(midState, null, 2) + '\n');
   978	    io.fs.writeFileSync(p.journal, JSON.stringify({ seq: 1, ts: T0, type: 'phase-advance' }) + '\n');
   979	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 999999, startTime: 7, runId: 'loop-prior' }));
   980

   982	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   983

   985	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 1, 'the completed plan phase is NOT re-run — only gate-1 spawns');
   986	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls[0].command, 'codex', 'the resumed child is the gate-1 reviewer (codex)');
   987	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'resuming the remaining phase completes the run');
   988	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   989	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE);
   990	  });
